
==================================================
FILE: CraftDailyCorner\Areas\Admin\ViewComponents\VCAdminSidebar.cs
==================================================

using CraftDailyCorner.Areas.Admin.ViewModels;
using CraftDailyCorner.Services.Interface;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace CraftDailyCorner.Areas.Admin.ViewComponents
{
    public class VCAdminSidebar : ViewComponent
    {
        private readonly IAdminSidebarService _sidebarService;

        public VCAdminSidebar(IAdminSidebarService sidebarService)
        {
            _sidebarService = sidebarService;
        }

        public async Task<IViewComponentResult> InvokeAsync()
        {
            var vm = await _sidebarService.GetSidebarDataAsync();
            return View(vm);
        }
    }
}

    Layout = "~/Areas/Admin/Views/Shared/_AdminLayout.cshtml";
}

<div class="container-fluid">

    <div class="mb-4">
        <h2 class="fw-bold text-light">新增公告</h2>
        <div class="text-light opacity-75">
            發布平台公告
        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
        <div class="card-body">

            <form asp-action="Create" method="post">

                <div class="mb-3">
                    <label asp-for="Title" class="form-label"></label>
                    <input asp-for="Title" class="form-control" />
                    <span asp-validation-for="Title" class="text-danger"></span>
                </div>

                <div class="mb-3">
                    <label asp-for="Content" class="form-label"></label>
                    <textarea asp-for="Content"
                              class="form-control"
                              rows="6"></textarea>
                    <span asp-validation-for="Content" class="text-danger"></span>
                </div>

                <div class="mb-3">
                    <label asp-for="AudienceType" class="form-label"></label>
                    <select asp-for="AudienceType"
                            asp-items="Model.AudienceOptions"
                            class="form-select"></select>
                </div>

                <div class="mb-4">
                    <label asp-for="StatusID" class="form-label"></label>
                    <select asp-for="StatusID"
                            asp-items="Model.StatusOptions"
                            class="form-select"></select>
                </div>

                <div class="d-flex gap-2">

                    <button type="submit"
                            class="btn btn-primary">
                        <i class="bi bi-save"></i>
                        儲存
                    </button>

                    <a asp-action="Index"
                       class="btn btn-outline-secondary">
                        返回列表
                    </a>

                </div>

            </form>

        </div>
    </div>
</div>

FILE: CraftDailyCorner\Areas\Admin\Views\Announcement\Detail.cshtml

@model CraftDailyCorner.Areas.Admin.ViewModels.Announcement.VMAdminAnnouncementDetail

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "公告詳情";
    Layout = "~/Areas/Admin/Views/Shared/_AdminLayout.cshtml";
}

<div class="container-fluid">

    @if (TempData["Success"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-success">@TempData["Success"]</div>
    }

    @if (TempData["Warning"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-warning">@TempData["Warning"]</div>
    }

    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
        <div>
            <h2 class="fw-bold text-light mb-1">公告詳情</h2>
            <div class="text-light opacity-75">
                公告 ID：@Model.AnnouncementID
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="d-flex gap-2">
            <a asp-action="Edit"
               asp-route-id="@Model.AnnouncementID"
               class="btn btn-outline-primary">
                <i class="bi bi-pencil"></i>
                編輯
            </a>

            <a asp-action="Index"
               class="btn btn-outline-secondary">
                返回列表
            </a>
        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card shadow-sm border-0 mb-4">
        <div class="card-body">

            <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-start flex-wrap gap-3 mb-3">
                <div>
                    <h3 class="fw-bold mb-2">@Model.Title</h3>

                    <div class="d-flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                        <span class="badge bg-info text-dark">@Model.AudienceName</span>

                        @if (Model.StatusID == 2)
                        {
                            <span class="badge bg-success">@Model.StatusName</span>
                        }
                        else if (Model.StatusID == 1)
                        {
                            <span class="badge bg-secondary">@Model.StatusName</span>
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            <span class="badge bg-dark">@Model.StatusName</span>
                        }
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <hr />

            <div class="mb-4">
                <div class="text-muted mb-2">公告內容</div>
                <div style="white-space: pre-wrap;">@Model.Content</div>
            </div>

            <hr />

            <div class="row g-3">
                <div class="col-md-6">
                    <div class="text-muted small">建立時間</div>
                    <div>@Model.CreatedAt.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm")</div>
                </div>

                <div class="col-md-6">
                    <div class="text-muted small">建立者</div>
                    <div>@(Model.CreatedByName ?? Model.CreatedBy)</div>
                </div>

                <div class="col-md-6">
                    <div class="text-muted small">最後更新時間</div>
                    <div>@(Model.UpdatedAt?.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm") ?? "-")</div>
                </div>

                <div class="col-md-6">
                    <div class="text-muted small">最後更新者</div>
                    <div>@(Model.UpdatedByName ?? Model.UpdatedBy ?? "-")</div>
                </div>

                <div class="col-md-6">
                    <div class="text-muted small">發布時間</div>
                    <div>@(Model.PublishedAt?.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm") ?? "-")</div>
                </div>
            </div>

        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="d-flex gap-2">
        @if (Model.StatusID != 2)
        {
            <form asp-action="Activate" method="post">
                @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                <input type="hidden" name="id" value="@Model.AnnouncementID" />
                <button type="submit" class="btn btn-success">
                    <i class="bi bi-check-circle"></i>
                    啟用公告
                </button>
            </form>
        }
        else
        {
            <form asp-action="Inactivate" method="post">
                @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                <input type="hidden" name="id" value="@Model.AnnouncementID" />
                <button type="submit" class="btn btn-danger">
                    <i class="bi bi-x-circle"></i>
                    停用公告
                </button>
            </form>
        }
    </div>
</div>

                        <i class="bi bi-save"></i>
                        儲存變更
                    </button>

                       asp-route-id="@Model.AnnouncementID"
                       class="btn btn-outline-secondary">
                        返回詳情
                    </a>

                    <a asp-action="Index"
                       class="btn btn-outline-light">
                        返回列表
                    </a>
                </div>
            </form>

        </div>
    </div>
</div>

FILE: CraftDailyCorner\Areas\Admin\Views\Announcement\Index.cshtml
==================================================

@model CraftDailyCorner.Areas.Admin.ViewModels.Announcement.VMAdminAnnouncementIndex

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "公告管理";
    Layout = "~/Areas/Admin/Views/Shared/_AdminLayout.cshtml";
}

<div class="container-fluid">

    @if (TempData["Success"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-success">@TempData["Success"]</div>
    }

    @if (TempData["Warning"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-warning">@TempData["Warning"]</div>
    }

    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-end mb-4">
        <div>
            <h2 class="fw-bold mb-1 text-light">公告管理</h2>
            <div class="text-light opacity-75">管理平台公告內容與發布狀態</div>
        </div>

        <a asp-area="Admin"
           asp-controller="Announcement"
           asp-action="Create"
           class="btn btn-primary">
            <i class="bi bi-plus-circle me-1"></i> 新增公告
        </a>
    </div>

    <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
        <div class="card-body p-0">

            @if (Model.Items == null || !Model.Items.Any())
            {
                <div class="p-4 text-secondary">
                    目前尚無公告資料
                </div>
            }
            else
            {
                <div class="table-responsive">
                    <table class="table table-dark table-hover align-middle mb-0">
                        <thead>
                            <tr>
                                <th style="width:100px;">公告ID</th>
                                <th>標題</th>
                                <th style="width:140px;">受眾</th>
                                <th style="width:120px;">狀態</th>
                                <th style="width:180px;">發布時間</th>
                                <th style="width:160px;">建立者</th>
                                <th style="width:260px;" class="text-end"></th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>

                        <tbody>
                            @foreach (var item in Model.Items)
                            {
                                string statusBadgeClass = item.StatusID switch
                                {
                                    2 => "bg-success",
                                    1 => "bg-secondary",
                                    _ => "bg-dark"
                                };

                                string statusText = item.StatusID switch
                                {
                                    2 => "啟用",
                                    1 => "草稿",
                                    _ => "停用"
                                };

                                string toggleBtnText = item.StatusID == 2 ? "停用" : "啟用";
                                string toggleBtnClass = item.StatusID == 2 ? "btn-outline-danger" : "btn-outline-success";
                                string toggleAction = item.StatusID == 2 ? "Inactivate" : "Activate";

                                <tr>
                                    <td>#@item.AnnouncementID</td>

                                    <td>
                                        <div class="fw-semibold">@item.Title</div>
                                    </td>

                                    <td>@item.AudienceName</td>

                                    <td>
                                        <span class="badge @statusBadgeClass">
                                            @statusText
                                        </span>
                                    </td>

                                    <td>
                                        @(item.PublishedAt?.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm") ?? "-")
                                    </td>

                                    <td>
                                        @(item.CreatedByName ?? item.CreatedBy)
                                    </td>

                                    <td class="text-end">
                                        <a asp-area="Admin"
                                           asp-controller="Announcement"
                                           asp-action="Detail"
                                           asp-route-id="@item.AnnouncementID"
                                           class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-light">
                                            查看
                                        </a>

                                        <a asp-area="Admin"
                                           asp-controller="Announcement"
                                           asp-action="Edit"
                                           asp-route-id="@item.AnnouncementID"
                                           class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary">
                                            編輯
                                        </a>

                                        <form class="d-inline"
                                              asp-area="Admin"
                                              asp-controller="Announcement"
                                              asp-action="@toggleAction"
                                              method="post">
                                            @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                                            <input type="hidden"
                                                   name="id"
                                                   value="@item.AnnouncementID" />
                                            <button type="submit" class="btn btn-sm @toggleBtnClass">
                                                @toggleBtnText
                                            </button>
                                        </form>
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            }
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
            }

        </div>
    </div>
</div>

@model CraftDailyCorner.Areas.Admin.ViewModels.Category.VMAdminCategoryUpsert

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "新增分類";
    Layout = "~/Areas/Admin/Views/Shared/_AdminLayout.cshtml";
}

<div class="container-fluid">

    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-end mb-4">
        <div>
            <h2 class="fw-bold mb-1 text-light">新增分類</h2>
            <div class="text-light opacity-75">可新增大分類或小分類（選擇上層即為小分類）</div>
        </div>

        <a class="btn btn-outline-light"
           asp-area="Admin"
           asp-controller="Categories"
           asp-action="Index">
            返回列表
        </a>
    </div>

    <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
        <div class="card-body">

            <form asp-area="Admin"
                  asp-controller="Categories"
                  asp-action="Create"
                  method="post">
                @Html.AntiForgeryToken()

                <div class="mb-3">
                    <label asp-for="CategoryName" class="form-label text-light"></label>
                    <input asp-for="CategoryName" class="form-control" />
                    <span asp-validation-for="CategoryName" class="text-danger"></span>
                </div>

                <div class="mb-3">
                    <label asp-for="ParentCategoryID" class="form-label text-light"></label>
                    <select asp-for="ParentCategoryID"
                            asp-items="Model.ParentCategoryOptions"
                            class="form-select">
                    </select>
                    <div class="form-text text-light opacity-75">
                        選「（建立/設為大分類）」代表 ParentCategoryID = null。
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div class="form-check form-switch mb-4">
                    <input asp-for="IsActive" class="form-check-input" />
                    <label asp-for="IsActive" class="form-check-label text-light"></label>
                </div>

                <div class="d-flex gap-2">
                    <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary">
                        <i class="bi bi-check2-circle me-1"></i> 新增
                    </button>

                    <a class="btn btn-outline-light"
                       asp-area="Admin"
                       asp-controller="Categories"
                       asp-action="Index">
                        取消
                    </a>
                </div>

            </form>

        </div>
    </div>

</div>

@section Scripts {
    @{
        await Html.RenderPartialAsync("_ValidationScriptsPartial");
    }
}

==================================================
FILE: CraftDailyCorner\Areas\Admin\Views\Categories\Index.cshtml
==================================================

@model CraftDailyCorner.Areas.Admin.ViewModels.Category.VMAdminCategoryIndex

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "分類管理";
    Layout = "~/Areas/Admin/Views/Shared/_AdminLayout.cshtml";
}

<div class="container-fluid">

    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-end mb-4">
        <div>
            <h2 class="fw-bold mb-1 text-light">分類管理</h2>
            <div class="text-light opacity-75">新增 / 修改 / 刪除大分類與小分類</div>
        </div>

        <a class="btn btn-primary"
           asp-area="Admin"
           asp-controller="Categories"
           asp-action="Create">
            <i class="bi bi-plus-circle me-1"></i> 新增分類
        </a>
    </div>

    @if (TempData["Success"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-success">@TempData["Success"]</div>
    }
    @if (TempData["Warning"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-warning">@TempData["Warning"]</div>
    }

    <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
        <div class="card-body p-0">

            @if (!Model.Items.Any())
            {
                <div class="p-4 text-center text-light opacity-75">
                    <i class="bi bi-inbox fs-2"></i>
                    <div class="mt-2">目前沒有分類資料</div>
                </div>
            }
            else
            {
                <div class="table-responsive">
                    <table class="table table-dark table-hover align-middle mb-0">
                        <thead>
                            <tr>
                                <th style="width:120px;">ID</th>
                                <th>分類名稱</th>
                                <th style="width:220px;">上層（大分類）</th>
                                <th style="width:120px;">狀態</th>
                                <th style="width:200px;">建立時間</th>
                                <th style="width:220px;"></th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody>
                            @foreach (var x in Model.Items)
                            {
                                var isParent = x.Level == 0;

                                <tr class="@(isParent ? "table-active" : "")">
                                    <td>#@x.CategoryID</td>

                                    <td>
                                        @if (isParent)
                                        {
                                            <span class="fw-semibold">
                                                <i class="bi bi-folder2-open me-1"></i>@x.CategoryName
                                            </span>
                                        }
                                        else
                                        {
                                            <span class="ms-4">
                                                <i class="bi bi-caret-right-fill me-1"></i>@x.CategoryName
                                            </span>
                                        }
                                    </td>

                                    <td class="text-light opacity-75">
                                        @(x.ParentCategoryID == null ? "—" : x.ParentCategoryName)
                                    </td>

                                    <td>
                                        @if (x.IsActive)
                                        {
                                            <span class="badge bg-success">啟用</span>
                                        }
                                        else
                                        {
                                            <span class="badge bg-secondary">停用</span>
                                        }
                                    </td>

                                    <td class="text-light opacity-75">
                                        @x.CreatedAt.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm")
                                    </td>

                                    <td class="text-end">
                                        <a class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-light me-2"
                                           asp-area="Admin"
                                           asp-controller="Categories"
                                           asp-action="Edit"
                                           asp-route-id="@x.CategoryID">
                                            編輯
                                        </a>

                                        @if (x.IsActive)
                                        {
                                            <form asp-area="Admin"
                                                  asp-controller="Categories"
                                                  asp-action="Disable"
                                                  asp-route-id="@x.CategoryID"
                                                  method="post"
                                                  class="d-inline"
                                                  onsubmit="return confirm('確定要停用這個分類嗎？');">
                                                @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                                                <button type="submit" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-warning">
                                                    停用
                                                </button>
                                            </form>
                                        }
                                        else
                                        {
                                            <form asp-area="Admin"
                                                  asp-controller="Categories"
                                                  asp-action="Enable"
                                                  asp-route-id="@x.CategoryID"
                                                  method="post"
                                                  class="d-inline"
                                                  onsubmit="return confirm('確定要啟用這個分類嗎？');">
                                                @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                                                <button type="submit" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-success">
                                                    啟用
                                                </button>
                                            </form>
                                        }
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            }
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
            }

        </div>
    </div>

</div>

==================================================
FILE: CraftDailyCorner\Areas\Admin\Views\CreatorReview\Detail.cshtml
==================================================

@using CraftDailyCorner.Extensions
@model CraftDailyCorner.Areas.Admin.ViewModels.CreatorReview.VMAdminCreatorReviewDetail

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "申請詳情";
    Layout = "~/Areas/Admin/Views/Shared/_AdminLayout.cshtml";
    var currentMemberId = User.GetMemberId();
    var isSelfApplication = (Model.MemberID == currentMemberId);
}

<div class="container-fluid">

    @if (TempData["Danger"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-danger">@TempData["Danger"]</div>
    }
    @if (TempData["Success"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-success">@TempData["Success"]</div>
    }
    @if (TempData["Warning"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-warning">@TempData["Warning"]</div>
    }

    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-end mb-4">
        <div>
            <h2 class="fw-bold mb-1 text-light">申請詳情</h2>
            <div class="text-secondary">#@Model.ApplicationID</div>
        </div>

        <a class="btn btn-outline-secondary"
           asp-area="Admin"
           asp-controller="CreatorReview"
           asp-action="Index">
           返回列表
        </a>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-4">

        <!-- 左：申請內容 -->
        <div class="col-lg-7">
            <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
                <div class="card-body">

                    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-3">
                        <div class="fw-semibold text-light">申請內容</div>
                        @{
                            var badge = Model.StatusID == 1 ? "bg-danger"
                            : Model.StatusID == 2 ? "bg-success"
                            : Model.StatusID == 3 ? "bg-warning text-dark"
                            : "bg-secondary";
                        }
                        <span class="badge @badge">@Model.StatusName</span>
                    </div>
                    <hr/>
                    <div class="mb-3">
                        <div class="text-secondary small mb-1">品牌名稱</div>
                        <div class="fs-5 fw-bold text-light">@((Model.BrandName ?? "（未填）"))</div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="mb-3">
                        <div class="text-secondary small mb-1">品牌簡介</div>
                        <div class="text-light" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">@(Model.BrandIntro ?? "（未填）")</div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="row g-3 mb-3">
                        <div class="col-md-6">
                            <div class="text-secondary small mb-1">創作起始日期</div>
                            <div class="text-light">
                                @(Model.StartDate.HasValue? Model.StartDate.Value.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd") : "（未填）")
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="col-md-6">
                            <div class="text-secondary small mb-1">申請提交時間</div>
                            <div class="text-light">@Model.AppliedAt.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm")</div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="mb-3">
                        <div class="text-secondary small mb-2">作品</div>
                        @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Model.PortfolioUrl))
                        {
                            <img src="@($"/Photos/02CreatorApplication/Large/{Model.PortfolioUrl}.png")"
                                 alt="作品預覽"
                                 class="img-fluid rounded shadow-sm" />
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            <div class="text-secondary">（未上傳）</div>
                        }
                    </div>

                    <hr class="border-secondary" />

                    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">
                        <div class="fw-semibold text-light">審核資訊</div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="row g-3 mt-1">
                        <div class="col-md-6">
                            <div class="text-secondary small mb-1">審核人</div>
                            <div class="text-light">@((Model.ReviewerName ?? "（尚未審核）"))</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="col-md-6">
                            <div class="text-secondary small mb-1">審核時間</div>
                            <div class="text-light">
                                @(Model.ReviewedAt.HasValue? Model.ReviewedAt.Value.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm") : "（尚未審核）")
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="col-12">
                            <div class="text-secondary small mb-1">審核備註 / 未通過原因</div>
                            <div class="text-light" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">@(Model.ReviewNote ?? "（無）")</div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 右：會員資訊 + 操作 -->
        <div class="col-lg-5">

            <!-- 會員資訊 -->
            <div class="card shadow-sm border-0 mb-4">
                <div class="card-body">
                    <div class="fw-semibold text-light mb-3">
                        <i class="bi bi-person-badge me-2"></i>會員資訊
                    </div>

                        <div class="col-md-6">
                            <div class="text-secondary small mb-1">會員編號</div>
                            <div class="text-light">@Model.MemberID</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="col-md-6">
                            <div class="text-secondary small mb-1">會員名稱</div>
                            <div class="text-light">@Model.MemberName</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="col-12">
                            <div class="text-secondary small mb-1">Email</div>
                            <div class="text-light">@((Model.Email ?? "（未提供）"))</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="col-12">
                            <div class="text-secondary small mb-1">連絡電話</div>
                            <div class="text-light">@((Model.Phone ?? "（未提供）"))</div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="card shadow-sm border-0 mb-4">
                <div class="card-body">
                    <div class="fw-semibold text-light mb-3">
                        <i class="bi bi-shield-check me-2"></i>審核操作
                    </div>

                    @* 只有待審核（StatusID=1）才可操作 *@
                    @if (Model.StatusID == 1 && !isSelfApplication)
                    {
                        <form asp-area="Admin"
                              asp-controller="CreatorReview"
                              asp-action="Approve"
                              method="post"
                              class="mb-3">
                            @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                            <input type="hidden" name="id" value="@Model.ApplicationID" />

                            <label class="text-secondary small mb-1">通過備註（可不填）</label>
                            <textarea name="reviewNote"
                                  class="form-control bg-dark text-light border-secondary"
                                  rows="3"
                                  placeholder="例如：請補上品牌 Logo，通過後可至創作者中心設定..."></textarea>

                            <button type="submit" class="btn btn-success w-100 mt-3">
                                <i class="bi bi-check2-circle me-1"></i> 通過申請
                            </button>
                        </form>

                        <hr class="border-secondary" />

                        <form asp-area="Admin"
                              asp-controller="CreatorReview"
                              asp-action="Reject"
                              method="post">
                            @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                            <input type="hidden" name="id" value="@Model.ApplicationID" />

                            <label class="text-secondary small mb-1">未通過原因（必填）</label>
                            <textarea name="reviewNote"
                                  class="form-control bg-dark text-light border-secondary"
                                  rows="4"
                                  required
                                  placeholder="請說明未通過原因（例如：作品照片不清晰、品牌簡介不足...）"></textarea>

                            <button type="submit" class="btn btn-outline-warning w-100 mt-3">
                                <i class="bi bi-x-circle me-1"></i> 駁回申請
                            </button>
                        </form>
                        
                    }
                    else if (Model.StatusID == 1 && isSelfApplication)
                    {
                        <div class="alert alert-warning mb-0">
                            你不能審核自己的創作者申請。
                        </div>

                        <button class="btn btn-success w-100 mt-3" disabled>
                            <i class="bi bi-check2-circle me-1"></i> 通過申請
                        </button>

                        <button class="btn btn-outline-warning w-100 mt-2" disabled>
                            <i class="bi bi-x-circle me-1"></i> 駁回申請
                        </button>
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        <div class="text-secondary">
                            此申請已完成審核，無法再次操作。
                        </div>

                        <div class="mt-3 p-3 rounded border border-secondary">
                            <div class="text-secondary small mb-1">目前狀態</div>
                            <div class="text-light fw-semibold">@Model.StatusName</div>
                        </div>
                        
                    }
                </div>
            </div>
            <!--下一筆待審核-->
            <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
                <div class="card-body">
                    <a asp-area="Admin"
                       asp-controller="CreatorReview"
                       asp-action="Next"
                       asp-route-id="@Model.ApplicationID"
                       class="btn btn-outline-info w-100 mt-3">
                        <i class="bi bi-skip-forward-circle me-1"></i> 下一筆待審核
                    </a>

                    <div class="text-secondary small mt-2">
                        若已沒有下一筆待審核，會自動返回列表
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

FILE: CraftDailyCorner\Areas\Admin\Views\CreatorReview\Index.cshtml

@model CraftDailyCorner.Areas.Admin.ViewModels.CreatorReview.VMAdminCreatorReviewIndex

@{
    var isHistory = Model.Mode == "history";

    ViewData["Title"] = isHistory
        ? "創作者審核（歷史資料）"
        : "創作者審核（待審核）";

    Layout = "~/Areas/Admin/Views/Shared/_AdminLayout.cshtml";
}

<div class="container-fluid">

    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-end mb-4">
        <div>
            <h2 class="fw-bold mb-1 text-light">創作者審核</h2>
            <div class="text-light opacity-75">
                @(isHistory
                                ? "歷史資料（已通過 / 已拒絕 / 已確認）"
                                : "待審核申請清單")
            </div>
        </div>

        @if (isHistory)
        {
            <!-- 右上角搜尋框：MemberID -->
            <form method="get"
                  asp-area="Admin"
                  asp-controller="CreatorReview"
                  asp-action="Index"
                  class="d-flex gap-2 align-items-center justify-content-center"
                  style="min-width:520px;">

                <input type="hidden" name="mode" value="history" />

                <div class="input-group" style="width:300px;">
                    <span class="input-group-text bg-dark text-light border-secondary">
                        <i class="bi bi-search"></i>
                    </span>

                    <input name="memberId"
                           value="@Model.SearchMemberId"
                           class="form-control bg-dark text-light border-secondary"
                           placeholder="輸入 MemberID" />
                </div>

                <button class="btn btn-outline-light" type="submit">
                    搜尋
                </button>

                <a class="btn btn-outline-secondary @(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Model.SearchMemberId) ? "invisible" : "")"
                   asp-area="Admin"
                   asp-controller="CreatorReview"
                   asp-action="Index"
                   asp-route-mode="history">
                    清除
                </a>
            </form>
        }
        else
        {
            <span class="badge bg-danger @(Model.Items.Count == 0 ? "d-none" : "")">
                待審核 @Model.Items.Count
            </span>
        }
    </div>

    <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
        <div class="card-body p-0">

            @if (!Model.Items.Any())
            {
                <div class="p-4 text-center text-light opacity-75">
                    <i class="bi bi-inbox fs-2"></i>
                    <div class="mt-2">
                        @(isHistory ? "查無資料" : "目前沒有待審核申請")
                    </div>
                </div>
            }
            else
            {
                <div class="table-responsive">
                    <table class="table table-dark table-hover align-middle mb-0">
                        <thead>
                            <tr>
                                <th style="width:120px;">申請ID</th>
                                <th>會員</th>
                                <th>申請名稱</th>
                                <th style="width:180px;">申請時間</th>
                                <th style="width:140px;">狀態</th>
                                <th style="width:120px;"></th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody>
                            @foreach (var x in Model.Items)
                            {
                                var rowClass = isHistory ? "" : "table-active";

                                var badge = !isHistory
                                ? "bg-danger"
                                : x.StatusID == 2 ? "bg-success"
                                : x.StatusID == 3 ? "bg-danger"
                                : x.StatusID == 4 ? "bg-primary"
                                : "bg-secondary";

                                <tr class="@rowClass">
                                    <td>#@x.ApplicationID</td>
                                    <td>
                                        <div class="fw-semibold">@x.MemberName</div>
                                        <div class="small text-light opacity-75">@x.Email / @x.Phone</div>
                                        <div class="small text-light opacity-75">@x.MemberID</div>
                                    </td>
                                    <td class="fw-semibold">@x.BrandName</td>
                                    <td>@x.AppliedAt.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm")</td>
                                    <td>
                                        <span class="badge @badge">@x.StatusName</span>
                                    </td>
                                    <td class="text-end">
                                        <a class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-light"
                                           asp-area="Admin"
                                           asp-controller="CreatorReview"
                                           asp-action="Detail"
                                           asp-route-id="@x.ApplicationID">
                                            查看
                                        </a>
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            }
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
            }

            @if (isHistory && Model.TotalPages > 1)
            {
                <div class="p-3 border-top border-secondary d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">
                    <div class="text-light opacity-75 small">
                        第 @Model.Page / @Model.TotalPages 頁，共 @Model.TotalCount 筆
                    </div>

                    <nav aria-label="Page navigation">
                        <ul class="pagination pagination-sm mb-0">

                            <li class="page-item @(Model.Page <= 1 ? "disabled" : "")">
                                <a class="page-link bg-dark text-light border-secondary"
                                   asp-area="Admin"
                                   asp-controller="CreatorReview"
                                   asp-action="Index"
                                   asp-route-mode="history"
                                   asp-route-memberId="@Model.SearchMemberId"
                                   asp-route-page="@(Model.Page - 1)">
                                    上一頁
                                </a>
                            </li>

                            @for (int p = 1; p <= Model.TotalPages; p++)
                            {
                                <li class="page-item @(p == Model.Page ? "active" : "")">
                                    <a class="page-link bg-dark text-light border-secondary"
                                       asp-area="Admin"
                                       asp-controller="CreatorReview"
                                       asp-action="Index"
                                       asp-route-mode="history"
                                       asp-route-memberId="@Model.SearchMemberId"
                                       asp-route-page="@p">
                                        @p
                                    </a>
                                </li>
                            }

                            <li class="page-item @(Model.Page >= Model.TotalPages ? "disabled" : "")">
                                <a class="page-link bg-dark text-light border-secondary"
                                   asp-area="Admin"
                                   asp-controller="CreatorReview"
                                   asp-action="Index"
                                   asp-route-mode="history"
                                   asp-route-memberId="@Model.SearchMemberId"
                                   asp-route-page="@(Model.Page + 1)">
                                    下一頁
                                </a>
                            </li>

                        </ul>
                    </nav>
                </div>
            }

        </div>
    </div>

</div>

                                <i class="bi bi-check2-circle me-1"></i> 新增
                            </button>

                            <a class="btn btn-outline-light"
                               asp-area="Admin"
                               asp-controller="HomepageBanner"
                               asp-action="Index">
                                取消
                            </a>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <!-- 右側：圖片預覽 -->
                    <div class="col-lg-6">
                        <div class="border rounded p-3 h-100 bg-dark-subtle">
                            <div class="fw-semibold text-light mb-3">
                                <i class="bi bi-image me-2"></i>圖片預覽
                            </div>

                            <div class="ratio ratio-16x9 border rounded overflow-hidden bg-dark d-flex align-items-center justify-content-center">
                                <img id="ImagePreview"
                                     src=""
                                     alt="圖片預覽"
                                     class="w-100 h-100 d-none"
                                     style="object-fit:cover;" />

                                <div id="PreviewPlaceholder"
                                     class="text-secondary text-center px-3">
                                    <i class="bi bi-card-image fs-1 d-block mb-2"></i>
                                    尚未選擇圖片
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div class="text-secondary small mt-3">
                                選擇圖片後，這裡會顯示預覽效果。
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

            </form>

        </div>
    </div>
</div>

@section Scripts {
    @{
        await Html.RenderPartialAsync("_ValidationScriptsPartial");
    }

    <script>
        (function () {
            const input = document.getElementById("ImageFile");
            const preview = document.getElementById("ImagePreview");
            const placeholder = document.getElementById("PreviewPlaceholder");

            if (!input || !preview || !placeholder) return;

            input.addEventListener("change", function () {
                const file = input.files && input.files[0];

                if (!file) {
                    preview.src = "";
                    preview.classList.add("d-none");
                    placeholder.classList.remove("d-none");
                    return;
                }

                const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);
                preview.src = objectUrl;
                preview.classList.remove("d-none");
                placeholder.classList.add("d-none");
            });
        })();
    </script>
}

@using CraftDailyCorner.Extensions
@model CraftDailyCorner.Areas.Admin.ViewModels.Member.VMAdminMemberDetail

@functions {
    public string MaskEmail(string? email)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(email) || !email.Contains("@"))
            return "（未提供）";

        var parts = email.Split('@');
        var name = parts[0];
        var domain = parts[1];

        if (name.Length <= 2)
            return $"{name[0]}***@{domain}";

        return $"{name.Substring(0, 2)}***@{domain}";
    }

    public string MaskPhone(string? phone)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(phone))
            return "（未提供）";

        var trimmed = phone.Trim();
        if (trimmed.Length <= 4)
            return "***";

        return $"{trimmed.Substring(0, 2)}***{trimmed.Substring(trimmed.Length - 2)}";
    }
}

    var mode = Context.Request.Query["mode"].ToString();

    var isSelf = Model.MemberID == currentMemberId;

}

                              asp-route-mode="@mode"
                              method="post"
                              onsubmit="return confirm('確定要停權這位會員嗎？');">

                            @Html.AntiForgeryToken()

                            <button class="btn btn-outline-warning w-100">
                                <i class="bi bi-pause-circle me-1"></i> 停權會員
                            </button>
                        </form>
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        <form asp-area="Admin"
                              asp-controller="Members"
                              asp-action="Activate"
                              asp-route-id="@Model.MemberID"
                              asp-route-mode="@mode"
                              method="post"
                              onsubmit="return confirm('確定要恢復這位會員嗎？');">

                            @Html.AntiForgeryToken()

                            <button class="btn btn-outline-success w-100">
                                <i class="bi bi-check2-circle me-1"></i> 恢復啟用
                            </button>
                        </form>
                    }
                </div>
            </div>

        </div>
    </div>
</div>

@using CraftDailyCorner.Extensions
@model CraftDailyCorner.Areas.Admin.ViewModels.Member.VMAdminMemberIndex

@{
    Layout = "~/Areas/Admin/Views/Shared/_AdminLayout.cshtml";
    var currentMemberId = User.GetMemberId();

    var title = Model.Mode == "risk"
        ? "會員管理"
        : Model.Mode == "admin"
            ? "管理管理者"
            : Model.Mode == "creator"
                ? "查看創作者資料"
                : "查看會員資料";

    var subtitle = Model.Mode == "risk"
        ? "高風險會員"
        : Model.Mode == "admin"
            ? "管理者帳號清單"
            : Model.Mode == "creator"
                ? "創作者清單"
                : "所有會員資料";

    var showSearch = Model.Mode == "all" || Model.Mode == "creator";
    var showPagination = Model.Mode == "all" || Model.Mode == "creator";
}

<div class="container-fluid">

    @if (TempData["Success"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-success">@TempData["Success"]</div>
    }
    @if (TempData["Warning"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-warning">@TempData["Warning"]</div>
    }

    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-end mb-4">
        <div>
            <h2 class="fw-bold mb-1 text-light">@title</h2>
            <div class="text-light opacity-75">@subtitle</div>
        </div>

        @if (showSearch)
        {
            <form method="get"
                  asp-area="Admin"
                  asp-controller="Members"
                  asp-action="Index"
                  class="d-flex gap-2 align-items-center justify-content-center"

                    <span class="input-group-text bg-dark text-light border-secondary">
                        <i class="bi bi-search"></i>
                    </span>

                           value="@Model.SearchMemberId"
                           class="form-control bg-dark text-light border-secondary"
                           placeholder="輸入 MemberID" />
                </div>

                <button class="btn btn-outline-light" type="submit">
                    搜尋
                </button>

                <a class="btn btn-outline-secondary @(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Model.SearchMemberId) ? "invisible" : "")"
                   asp-area="Admin"
                   asp-controller="Members"
                   asp-action="Index"
                   asp-route-mode="@Model.Mode">
                    清除
                </a>
            </form>
        }
    </div>

    <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
        <div class="card-body p-0">

            @if (!Model.Items.Any())
            {
                <div class="p-4 text-center text-light opacity-75">
                    <i class="bi bi-inbox fs-2"></i>
                    <div class="mt-2">目前沒有資料</div>
                </div>
            }
            else
            {
                <div class="table-responsive">
                    <table class="table table-dark table-hover align-middle mb-0">
                        <thead>
                            <tr>
                                <th style="width:140px;">@Html.DisplayNameFor(m => m.Items[0].MemberID)</th>
                                <th>@Html.DisplayNameFor(m => m.Items[0].DisplayName)</th>

                                @if (Model.Mode == "creator")
                                {
                                    <th style="width:120px;">@Html.DisplayNameFor(m => m.Items[0].CreatorID)</th>
                                    <th>@Html.DisplayNameFor(m => m.Items[0].BrandName)</th>
                                }

                                <th style="width:120px;">@Html.DisplayNameFor(m => m.Items[0].StatusName)</th>
                                @if (Model.Mode == "risk")
                                {
                                    <th style="width:140px;">@Html.DisplayNameFor(m => m.Items[0].ViolationCount)</th>
                                }

                                <th style="width:180px;">@Html.DisplayNameFor(m => m.Items[0].CreatedAt)</th>
                                <th style="width:220px;"></th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody>
                            @foreach (var x in Model.Items)
                            {
                                <tr class="@(Model.Mode == "risk" ? "table-active" : "")">
                                    <td>@x.MemberID</td>

                                    <td class="fw-semibold">@x.DisplayName</td>

                                    @if (Model.Mode == "creator")
                                    {
                                        <td>@(x.CreatorID ?? "—")</td>
                                        <td class="fw-semibold">@(x.BrandName ?? "—")</td>
                                    }

                                    <td>
                                        @if (x.StatusID == 1)
                                        {
                                            <span class="badge bg-success">@x.StatusName</span>
                                        }
                                        else
                                        {
                                            <span class="badge bg-secondary">@x.StatusName</span>
                                        }
                                    </td>

                                    @if (Model.Mode == "risk")
                                    {
                                        <td>
                                            <span class="badge bg-danger">@x.ViolationCount</span>
                                        </td>
                                    }

                                    <td>@x.CreatedAt.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm")</td>

                                    <td class="text-end">
                                        <a class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-light me-2"
                                           asp-area="Admin"
                                           asp-controller="Members"
                                           asp-action="Detail"
                                           asp-route-id="@x.MemberID"
                                           asp-route-mode="@Model.Mode">
                                            查看
                                        </a>

                                        @{
                                            var isSuperAdmin = x.RoleIDs.Contains("04");
                                        }

                                        @if (x.MemberID == currentMemberId)
                                        {
                                            <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary" disabled>
                                                不可停權自己
                                            </button>
                                        }
                                        else if (isSuperAdmin)
                                        {
                                            <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger" disabled>
                                                超級管理者不可停權
                                            </button>
                                        }
                                        else if (x.StatusID == 1)
                                        {
                                            <form class="d-inline"
                                                  asp-area="Admin"
                                                  asp-controller="Members"
                                                  asp-action="Suspend"
                                                  asp-route-id="@x.MemberID"
                                                  asp-route-mode="@Model.Mode"
                                                  method="post"
                                                  onsubmit="return confirm('確定要停權這位會員嗎？');">
                                                @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                                                <button type="submit" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-warning">
                                                    停權
                                                </button>
                                            </form>
                                        }
                                        else
                                        {
                                            <form class="d-inline"
                                                  asp-area="Admin"
                                                  asp-controller="Members"
                                                  asp-action="Activate"
                                                  asp-route-id="@x.MemberID"
                                                  asp-route-mode="@Model.Mode"
                                                  method="post"
                                                  onsubmit="return confirm('確定要恢復這位會員嗎？');">
                                                @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                                                <button type="submit" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-success">
                                                    啟用
                                                </button>
                                            </form>
                                        }
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            }
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
            }

            @if (showPagination && Model.TotalPages > 1)
            {
                <div class="p-3 border-top border-secondary d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">
                    <div class="text-light opacity-75 small">
                        第 @Model.Page / @Model.TotalPages 頁，共 @Model.TotalCount 筆
                    </div>

                    <nav aria-label="Page navigation">
                        <ul class="pagination pagination-sm mb-0">

                            <li class="page-item @(Model.Page <= 1 ? "disabled" : "")">
                                <a class="page-link bg-dark text-light border-secondary"
                                   asp-area="Admin"
                                   asp-controller="Members"
                                   asp-action="Index"
                                   asp-route-mode="@Model.Mode"
                                   asp-route-memberId="@Model.SearchMemberId"
                                   asp-route-page="@(Model.Page - 1)">
                                    上一頁
                                </a>
                            </li>

                            @for (int p = 1; p <= Model.TotalPages; p++)
                            {
                                <li class="page-item @(p == Model.Page ? "active" : "")">
                                    <a class="page-link bg-dark text-light border-secondary"
                                       asp-area="Admin"
                                       asp-controller="Members"
                                       asp-action="Index"
                                       asp-route-mode="@Model.Mode"
                                       asp-route-memberId="@Model.SearchMemberId"
                                       asp-route-page="@p">
                                        @p
                                    </a>
                                </li>
                            }

                            <li class="page-item @(Model.Page >= Model.TotalPages ? "disabled" : "")">
                                <a class="page-link bg-dark text-light border-secondary"
                                   asp-area="Admin"
                                   asp-controller="Members"
                                   asp-action="Index"
                                   asp-route-mode="@Model.Mode"
                                   asp-route-memberId="@Model.SearchMemberId"
                                   asp-route-page="@(Model.Page + 1)">
                                    下一頁
                                </a>
                            </li>

                        </ul>
                    </nav>
                </div>
            }

        </div>
    </div>
</div>

@model CraftDailyCorner.Areas.Admin.ViewModels.PlatformSetting.VMAdminPlatformSettingIndex

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "平台參數設定";
    Layout = "~/Areas/Admin/Views/Shared/_AdminLayout.cshtml";

    var groupedItems = Model.Items
        .GroupBy(x => new { x.CategoryID, x.CategoryName })
        .OrderBy(g => g.Key.CategoryID)
        .ToList();
}

<div class="container-fluid">

    @if (TempData["Success"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-success">@TempData["Success"]</div>
    }

    @if (TempData["Warning"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-warning">@TempData["Warning"]</div>
    }

    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-end mb-4">
        <div>
            <h2 class="fw-bold mb-1 text-light">平台參數設定</h2>
            <div class="text-light opacity-75">管理平台的基本設定與前台顯示參數</div>
        </div>
    </div>

    @if (!Model.Items.Any())
    {
        <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
            <div class="card-body p-4 text-center text-light opacity-75">
                <i class="bi bi-sliders fs-2"></i>
                <div class="mt-2">目前沒有平台參數資料</div>
            </div>
        </div>
    }
    else
    {
        @foreach (var group in groupedItems)
        {
            <div class="card shadow-sm border-0 mb-4">
                <div class="card-header bg-dark border-bottom border-secondary">
                    <div class="fw-semibold text-light">
                        <i class="bi bi-folder2-open me-2"></i>@group.Key.CategoryName
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div class="card-body p-0">
                    <div class="table-responsive">
                        <table class="table table-dark table-hover align-middle mb-0">
                            <thead>
                                <tr>
                                    <th style="width:280px;">參數說明</th>
                                    <th style="width:240px;">目前設定值</th>
                                    <th style="width:180px;">更新時間</th>
                                    <th style="width:160px;">更新者</th>
                                    <th style="width:120px;"></th>
                                </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <tbody>
                                @foreach (var x in group)
                                {
                                    <tr>
                                        <td>
                                            <div class="fw-semibold text-light">
                                                @(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(x.Description) ? "—" : x.Description)
                                            </div>
                                            <div class="small text-light opacity-50 mt-1">
                                                @x.SettingKey
                                            </div>
                                        </td>

                                        <td>
                                            @if (string.Equals(x.DataType, "bool", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                            {
                                                var isEnabled = string.Equals(x.SettingValue, "true", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

                                                if (isEnabled)
                                                {
                                                    <span class="badge bg-success me-2">開啟</span>
                                                }
                                                else
                                                {
                                                    <span class="badge bg-secondary me-2">關閉</span>
                                                }
                                            }
                                            else
                                            {
                                                <span class="badge bg-secondary me-2">@x.SettingValue</span>
                                            }

                                        </td>

                                        <td>
                                            @x.UpdatedAt.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm")
                                        </td>

                                        <td>
                                            <div>@(x.UpdatedByName ?? "—")</div>
                                            <div class="small text-light opacity-75">@x.UpdatedBy</div>
                                        </td>

                                        <td class="text-end">
                                            <a class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-light"
                                               asp-area="Admin"
                                               asp-controller="PlatformSetting"
                                               asp-action="Edit"
                                               asp-route-id="@x.SettingID">
                                                編輯
                                            </a>
                                        </td>
                                    </tr>
                                }
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>

            </div>
        }

@model CraftDailyCorner.Areas.Admin.ViewModels.VMAdminSidebar

@{
    var currentController = ViewContext.RouteData.Values["controller"]?.ToString();
    var currentAction = ViewContext.RouteData.Values["action"]?.ToString();
    var currentMode = Context.Request.Query["mode"].ToString();

    var isDashboardPage = currentController == "Dashboard";

    var isMemberPage = currentController == "Members";

    // Members 各子頁 active 判斷
    var memberMode = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(currentMode) ? "all" : currentMode;

    var isMemberAllPage = currentController == "Members" && currentAction == "Index" && memberMode == "all";
    var isMemberCreatorPage = currentController == "Members" && currentAction == "Index" && memberMode == "creator";
    var isMemberAdminPage = currentController == "Members" && currentAction == "Index" && memberMode == "admin";

    var isCreatorReviewPage = currentController == "CreatorReview";
    var isCreatorReviewPendingPage = currentController == "CreatorReview" && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(currentMode);
    var isCreatorReviewHistoryPage = currentController == "CreatorReview" && currentMode == "history";

    var isViolationPage = currentController == "Violation";
    var isViolationPendingPage = currentController == "Violation" && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(currentMode);
    var isViolationHistoryPage = currentController == "Violation" && currentMode == "history";

    var isCategoriesPage = currentController == "Categories";
    var isTagPage = currentController == "Tag";
    var isAnnouncementPage = currentController == "Announcement";

    var isSystemSettingPage = currentController == "SystemSetting"
        || currentController == "HomepageBanner"
        || currentController == "PlatformSetting";

    var isHomepageBannerPage = currentController == "HomepageBanner";
    var isPlatformSettingPage = currentController == "PlatformSetting";
}

<div class="admin-sidebar p-3">

    <ul class="nav flex-column">

        <li class="nav-item mb-3">
            <a asp-area="Admin"
               asp-controller="Dashboard"
               asp-action="Index"

                儀錶板
            </a>
        </li>

                   asp-action="Index"

                        type="button"

    }

        <div>

           asp-area="Admin"

        </a>
    </div>

                </div>
            }
            else
            {
                <div class="table-responsive">
                    <table class="table table-dark table-hover align-middle mb-0">
                        <thead>
                            <tr>
                                <th style="width:120px;">TagID</th>
                                <th>標籤名稱</th>
                                <th style="width:120px;">狀態</th>
                                <th style="width:220px;" class="text-end"></th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody>
                            @foreach (var x in Model.Items)
                            {
                                var badge = x.IsActive ? "bg-success" : "bg-secondary";
                                var btnText = x.IsActive ? "禁用" : "啟用";
                                var btnClass = x.IsActive ? "btn-outline-warning" : "btn-outline-success";

                                <tr>
                                    <td>#@x.TagID</td>
                                    <td class="fw-semibold">@x.TagName</td>
                                    <td>
                                        <span class="badge @badge">
                                            @(x.IsActive ? "啟用" : "禁用")
                                        </span>
                                    </td>
                                    <td class="text-end">
                                        <a class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-light"
                                           asp-area="Admin"
                                           asp-controller="Tag"
                                           asp-action="Edit"
                                           asp-route-id="@x.TagID">
                                            編輯
                                        </a>

                                        <form class="d-inline"
                                              asp-area="Admin"
                                              asp-controller="Tag"
                                              asp-action="Toggle"
                                              asp-route-id="@x.TagID"
                                              method="post">
                                            @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                                            <button type="submit" class="btn btn-sm @btnClass">
                                                @btnText
                                            </button>
                                        </form>
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            }
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
            }

        </div>
    </div>
</div>

@using CraftDailyCorner.Extensions
@using CraftDailyCorner.Models.enums;
@model CraftDailyCorner.Areas.Admin.ViewModels.Violation.VMAdminViolationDetail

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "檢舉詳情";
    Layout = "~/Areas/Admin/Views/Shared/_AdminLayout.cshtml";
    var isSelfReport = Model.ReporterMemberID == User.GetMemberId();
}

<div class="container-fluid">

    @if (TempData["Success"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-success">@TempData["Success"]</div>
    }
    @if (TempData["Warning"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-warning">@TempData["Warning"]</div>
    }

    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-end mb-4">
        <div>
            <h2 class="fw-bold mb-1 text-light">檢舉詳情</h2>
