        </div>

        <a class="btn btn-outline-secondary"
           asp-area="Admin"
           asp-controller="Violation"
           asp-action="Index">
            返回列表
        </a>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-4">

        <!-- 左：檢舉內容 -->
        <div class="col-lg-7">
            <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
                <div class="card-body">

                    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-3">
                        <div class="fw-semibold text-light">檢舉內容</div>
                        @{
                            var badge = Model.StatusID == 1 ? "bg-danger"
                            : Model.StatusID == 2 ? "bg-danger"
                            : Model.StatusID == 3 ? "bg-success"
                            : "bg-secondary";
                        }
                        <span class="badge @badge">@Model.StatusName</span>
                    </div>

                    <hr />

                    <div class="row g-3">
                        <div class="col-md-6">
                            <div class="text-secondary small mb-1">建立時間</div>
                            <div class="text-light fw-semibold">@Model.CreatedAt.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm")</div>
                        </div>

                        <div class="col-md-6">
                            <div class="text-secondary small mb-1">檢舉原因</div>
                            <div class="text-light fw-semibold">@Model.ReasonName</div>

                            @if (Model.Reason == (int)ReportReason.Other && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Model.UserReasonText))
                            {
                                <div class="mt-2 p-3 rounded border border-secondary bg-dark text-light"
                                     style="white-space:pre-wrap;">
                                    @Model.UserReasonText
                                </div>
                            }
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <hr />

                    <div class="row g-3">
                        <div class="col-md-6">

                            <div class="mb-3">
                                <div class="text-secondary small mb-1">檢舉人</div>
                                <div class="text-light fw-semibold">@Model.ReporterName</div>
                                <div class="text-secondary small">@Model.ReporterMemberID</div>

                                @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Model.ReporterEmail) || !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Model.ReporterPhone))
                                {
                                    <div class="text-secondary small mt-1">
                                        @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Model.ReporterEmail))
                                        {
                                            <span>@Model.ReporterEmail</span>
                                        }
                                        @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Model.ReporterPhone))
                                        {
                                            <span> @Model.ReporterPhone</span>
                                        }
                                    </div>
                                }
                            </div>

                            <div class="mb-3">
                                <div class="text-secondary small mb-1">被檢舉目標</div>
                                <div class="text-light fw-semibold">@Model.TargetTypeName</div>
                                <div class="text-secondary small">@Model.TargetID</div>
                            </div>

                            <div class="mb-0">
                                <div class="text-secondary small mb-1">擁有者</div>
                                @if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Model.TargetOwnerID))
                                {
                                    <div class="text-light fw-semibold">@Model.TargetOwnerName</div>
                                    <div class="text-secondary small">@Model.TargetOwnerID</div>
                                }
                                else
                                {
                                    <div class="text-light opacity-75">（未知）</div>
                                }
                            </div>

                        </div>

                        <div class="col-md-6">
                            @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Model.TargetUrl))
                            {
                                <div class="mb-3">
                                    <div class="text-secondary small mb-2">快速前往</div>
                                    <a class="btn btn-outline-info w-100"
                                       href="@Model.TargetUrl"
                                       target="_blank"
                                       rel="noopener noreferrer">
                                        <i class="bi bi-box-arrow-up-right me-1"></i> 前往被檢舉目標（新分頁）
                                    </a>
                                    <div class="text-secondary small mt-2">
                                        ※ 會另開新分頁，不影響目前審核流程
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            }
                            else
                            {
                                <div class="text-secondary small">
                                    目前無法產生目標連結（可能缺少路由資訊或目標已不存在）
                                </div>
                            }
                        </div>
                    </div>

                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 右：審核操作 -->
        <div class="col-lg-5">
            <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
                <div class="card-body">
                    <div class="fw-semibold text-light mb-2">審核操作</div>
                    <div class="text-secondary small mb-3">請判斷是否違規，系統會依你選擇更新檢舉狀態與目標狀態。</div>

                    @if (Model.StatusID == 1 && !isSelfReport)
                    {
                        <div class="mb-3">
                            <label for="adminNote" class="text-secondary small mb-1">
                                管理者備註
                                <span id="noteRequiredHint" class="text-danger ms-1 d-none">（判定違規必填）</span>
                            </label>

                            <textarea id="adminNote"
                                  class="form-control bg-dark text-light border-secondary"
                                  rows="4"
                                  placeholder="例如：違規原因判定依據、補充說明..."></textarea>

                            <div class="text-secondary small mt-2">
                                ※ 判定違規時必填；判定正常可選填
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <!-- 判定正常：可勾選是否為惡意檢舉 -->
                        <div class="form-check mb-3">
                            <input class="form-check-input"
                                   type="checkbox"
                                   id="isMalicious"
                                   form="formMarkNormal"
                                   name="isMalicious"
                                   value="true" />
                            <label class="form-check-label text-light" for="isMalicious">
                                此檢舉屬於惡意檢舉
                            </label>
                            <div class="text-secondary small mt-1">
                                僅在檢舉內容明顯失實、濫用檢舉機制時勾選。
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <form id="formMarkViolation"
                              asp-area="Admin"
                              asp-controller="Violation"
                              asp-action="MarkViolation"
                              method="post"
                              class="mb-2">
                            @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                            <input type="hidden" name="id" value="@Model.ReportID" />
                            <input type="hidden" name="adminNote" id="adminNoteHiddenViolation" value="" />

                            <button type="submit"
                                    class="btn btn-danger w-100"
                                    id="btnMarkViolation">
                                <i class="bi bi-shield-exclamation me-1"></i> 判定違規
                            </button>
                        </form>

                        <form id="formMarkNormal"
                              asp-area="Admin"
                              asp-controller="Violation"
                              asp-action="MarkNormal"
                              method="post">
                            @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                            <input type="hidden" name="id" value="@Model.ReportID" />
                            <input type="hidden" name="adminNote" id="adminNoteHiddenNormal" value="" />

                            <button type="submit"
                                    class="btn btn-outline-success w-100"
                                    id="btnMarkNormal">
                                <i class="bi bi-check2-circle me-1"></i> 判定正常（無違規）
                            </button>
                        </form>

                        <script>
                            (function () {
                                const textarea = document.getElementById("adminNote");
                                const hiddenViolation = document.getElementById("adminNoteHiddenViolation");
                                const hiddenNormal = document.getElementById("adminNoteHiddenNormal");

                                const formViolation = document.getElementById("formMarkViolation");
                                const formNormal = document.getElementById("formMarkNormal");

                                const hint = document.getElementById("noteRequiredHint");
                                const maliciousCheckbox = document.getElementById("isMalicious");

                                function sync() {
                                    const v = textarea.value || "";
                                    hiddenViolation.value = v;
                                    hiddenNormal.value = v;
                                }

                                textarea.addEventListener("input", function () {
                                    sync();
                                    if ((textarea.value || "").trim()) {
                                        textarea.classList.remove("is-invalid");
                                        hint.classList.add("d-none");
                                    }
                                });

                                sync();

                                formViolation.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
                                    sync();
                                    const note = (textarea.value || "").trim();

                                    if (!note) {
                                        e.preventDefault();
                                        hint.classList.remove("d-none");
                                        textarea.classList.add("is-invalid");
                                        textarea.focus();
                                        return;
                                    }

                                    hint.classList.add("d-none");
                                    textarea.classList.remove("is-invalid");
                                });

                                formNormal.addEventListener("submit", function () {
                                    sync();
                                    hint.classList.add("d-none");
                                    textarea.classList.remove("is-invalid");
                                });

                                document.getElementById("btnMarkViolation").addEventListener("click", function () {
                                    hint.classList.remove("d-none");
                                });

                                document.getElementById("btnMarkNormal").addEventListener("click", function () {
                                    hint.classList.add("d-none");
                                });
                            })();
                        </script>
                    }
                    else if (Model.StatusID == 1 && isSelfReport)
                    {
                        <div class="alert alert-warning mb-0">
                            你不能審核自己提交的檢舉。
                        </div>

                        <button class="btn btn-danger w-100 mt-3" disabled>
                            <i class="bi bi-shield-exclamation me-1"></i> 判定違規
                        </button>

                        <button class="btn btn-outline-success w-100 mt-2" disabled>
                            <i class="bi bi-check2-circle me-1"></i> 判定正常（無違規）
                        </button>
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        <div class="text-secondary">
                            此檢舉已完成審核，無法再次操作。
                        </div>

                        @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Model.AdminNote))
                        {
                            <div class="mt-3 p-3 rounded border border-secondary">
                                <div class="text-secondary small mb-1">管理者備註</div>
                                <div class="text-light" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">@Model.AdminNote</div>
                            </div>
                        }
                    }

                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- 下一筆待審核 -->
            <div class="card shadow-sm border-0 mt-4">
                <div class="card-body">
                    <a asp-area="Admin"
                       asp-controller="Violation"
                       asp-action="Next"
                       asp-route-id="@Model.ReportID"
                       class="btn btn-outline-info w-100">
                        <i class="bi bi-skip-forward-circle me-1"></i> 下一筆待審核
                    </a>

                    <div class="text-secondary small mt-2">
                        若已沒有下一筆待審核，會自動返回列表
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

        </div>
    </div>
</div>

FILE: CraftDailyCorner\ViewComponents\VCCategoryDropdown.cs
==================================================

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using CraftDailyCorner.Models; 
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
namespace CraftDailyCorner.ViewComponents
{
    public class VCCategoryDropdown : ViewComponent
    {



        private readonly CraftDailyCornerContext _context;

        public VCCategoryDropdown(CraftDailyCornerContext context)
        {
            _context = context;
        }

        public async Task<IViewComponentResult> InvokeAsync()
        {
            var categories = await _context.Categories
                .Where(c => c.IsActive)
                .OrderBy(c => c.ParentCategoryID)
                .ThenBy(c => c.CategoryID)
                .ToListAsync();

            return View(categories);
        }
    }




}

FILE: CraftDailyCorner\ViewComponents\VCCreatorPost.cs
==================================================

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using CraftDailyCorner.Models;
using CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.Homepage;
namespace CraftDailyCorner.ViewComponents
{
    public class VCCreatorPost : ViewComponent
    {
        private readonly CraftDailyCornerContext _context;

        public VCCreatorPost(CraftDailyCornerContext context)
        {
            _context = context;
        }

        public async Task<IViewComponentResult> InvokeAsync()
        {

            var HPost = await _context.CreatorPosts
            .Where(p => p.StatusID == 1 && p.Visibility == 0)
            .OrderByDescending(p => p.CreatedAt)
            .Take(4)
            .Select(p => new VMHotPostCard
            {
                PostID = p.PostID,
                Title = p.Title,
                ImageUrl = p.ImageUrl,
                CreatedAt= p.CreatedAt,
                BrandName = p.CreatorProfile.BrandName,
                CreatorID = p.CreatorID


            })
            .ToListAsync();
            return View(HPost);
        }
    }
}

FILE: CraftDailyCorner\ViewComponents\VCHomepageBanner.cs
==================================================

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using CraftDailyCorner.Models;
using CraftDailyCorner.Services.Interface;
using CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.Homepage;

namespace CraftDailyCorner.ViewComponents
{
    public class VCHomepageBanner : ViewComponent
    {
        private readonly CraftDailyCornerContext _context;
        private readonly ISiteSettingService _siteSetting;

        public VCHomepageBanner(
            CraftDailyCornerContext context,
            ISiteSettingService siteSetting)
        {
            _context = context;
            _siteSetting = siteSetting;
        }

        public async Task<IViewComponentResult> InvokeAsync()
        {
            var autoplaySeconds = await _siteSetting.GetIntAsync("BannerAutoplaySeconds");

            if (autoplaySeconds <= 0)
                autoplaySeconds = 5;

            var items = await _context.HomepageBanners
                .Where(p => p.StatusID == 1)
                .OrderByDescending(p => p.CreatedAt)
                .Select(p => new VMHomepageBannerItem
                {
                    Title = p.Title,
                    Subtitle = p.Subtitle,
                    ImageUrl = p.ImageUrl
                })
                .ToListAsync();

            var vm = new VMHomepageBanner
            {
                AutoplaySeconds = autoplaySeconds,
                Items = items
            };

            return View(vm);
        }
    }
}

==================================================
FILE: CraftDailyCorner\ViewComponents\VCHotProducts.cs
==================================================

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using CraftDailyCorner.Models;
using CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.Homepage;
using CraftDailyCorner.Services.Interface;

namespace CraftDailyCorner.ViewComponents
{
    public class VCHotProducts : ViewComponent
    {
        private readonly CraftDailyCornerContext _context;
        private readonly ISiteSettingService _siteSetting;

        public VCHotProducts(CraftDailyCornerContext context, ISiteSettingService siteSetting)
        {
            _context = context;
            _siteSetting = siteSetting;
        }

        public async Task<IViewComponentResult> InvokeAsync()
        {
            var featuredCount = await _siteSetting.GetIntAsync("HomepageFeaturedProductCount");

            if (featuredCount <= 0)
                featuredCount = 4;

            var hotProducts = await _context.Products
                .Where(p => p.StatusID == 2)
                .Select(p => new VMHotProductCard
                {
                    ProductID = p.ProductID,
                    ProductName = p.ProductName,
                    Price = p.Price,
                    FavoriteCount = _context.FavoriteProducts
                        .Count(f => f.ProductID == p.ProductID),
                    CoverImage = _context.ProductImages
                        .Where(i => i.ProductID == p.ProductID && i.StatusID == 1)
                        .OrderBy(i => i.SortOrder)
                        .Select(i => i.ImageUrl)
                        .FirstOrDefault(),
                    BrandName = p.CreatorProfile.BrandName
                })
                .OrderByDescending(x => x.FavoriteCount)
                .Take(featuredCount)
                .ToListAsync();

            return View(hotProducts);
        }
    }
}

==================================================
FILE: CraftDailyCorner\ViewComponents\VCNotificationDropdown.cs
==================================================

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using CraftDailyCorner.Services.Interface;
using CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.Notification;
using CraftDailyCorner.Extensions;

namespace CraftDailyCorner.ViewComponents
{
    public class VCNotificationDropdown : ViewComponent
    {
        private readonly INotificationService _notificationService;

        public VCNotificationDropdown(INotificationService notificationService)
        {
            _notificationService = notificationService;
        }

        public async Task<IViewComponentResult> InvokeAsync()
        {
            var memberId = UserClaimsPrincipal?.GetMemberId();

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(memberId))
            {
                return View(new VMNotificationDropdown());
            }

            var unreadCount = await _notificationService.GetUnreadCountAsync(memberId);

            var items = await _notificationService.GetRecentAsync(memberId, 5);

            var vm = new VMNotificationDropdown
            {
                UnreadCount = unreadCount,
                Items = items
            };

            return View(vm);
        }
    }
}

==================================================
FILE: CraftDailyCorner\ViewComponents\VCPostComment.cs
==================================================

using CraftDailyCorner.Extensions;
using CraftDailyCorner.Services.Interface;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;

namespace CraftDailyCorner.ViewComponents
{
    public class VCPostComment : ViewComponent
    {
        private readonly ICreatorPostCommentService _service;

        public VCPostComment(
            ICreatorPostCommentService service)
        {
            _service = service;
        }

        public async Task<IViewComponentResult> InvokeAsync(string postId)
        {
            var user = HttpContext.User;

            string? memberId = null;
            string? creatorId = null;

            if (user.Identity?.IsAuthenticated == true)
            {
                memberId = user.GetMemberId();

                if (user.IsInRole("02"))
                {
                    creatorId = user.GetCreatorId();
                }
            }

            var comments = await _service
                .GetPostCommentsAsync(
                    postId,
                    memberId,
                    creatorId);

            return View(comments);
        }
    }
}

==================================================
FILE: CraftDailyCorner\ViewComponents\VCProductReview.cs
==================================================

using CraftDailyCorner.Models;
using CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.Product;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace CraftDailyCorner.ViewComponents
{
    public class VCProductReview : ViewComponent
    {
        private readonly CraftDailyCornerContext _context;

        public VCProductReview(CraftDailyCornerContext context)
        {
            _context = context;
        }

        public async Task<IViewComponentResult> InvokeAsync(string productID)
        {
            var reviews = await _context.ProductReviews
                .Where(r => r.ProductID == productID /*&& r.StatusID == 1*/)
                .OrderByDescending(r => r.CreatedAt)
                .Select(r => new VMProductReview
                {
                    MemberName = r.Member.DisplayName,
                    Rating = r.Rating,
                    Comment = r.Comment ?? "",
                    CreatedAt = r.CreatedAt
                })
                .ToListAsync();

            var vm = new VMProductReviewList
            {
                ProductID = productID,
                TotalCount = reviews.Count,
                AvgRating = reviews.Any() ? reviews.Average(r => r.Rating) : 0,
                Reviews = reviews
            };

            return View(vm);
        }
    }

}

@model CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.CreatorApplication.VMApprovedConfirm
@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "創作者資料確認";
}

<div class="container py-5" style="max-width:820px;">

    <div class="text-center mb-4">
        <i class="bi bi-award-fill text-success" style="font-size:3rem;"></i>
        <h2 class="fw-bold mt-2">恭喜審核通過！</h2>
        <p class="text-muted mb-0">完成最後資料填寫即可開通創作者功能</p>
    </div>

    <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
        <div class="card-body p-4">

            <form asp-action="ApprovedConfirm" method="post" enctype="multipart/form-data">
                @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                <input type="hidden" asp-for="ApplicationID" />

                <div class="text-danger mb-3">
                    @Html.ValidationSummary(false)
                </div>

                <!-- ===== 唯讀資訊 ===== -->
                <div class="mb-3 text-start">
                    <label class="form-label fw-semibold">品牌名稱</label>
                    <input class="form-control" value="@Model.BrandName" readonly />
                </div>

                <div class="mb-3 text-start">
                    <label class="form-label fw-semibold">品牌簡介</label>
                    <textarea class="form-control" rows="4" readonly>@Model.BrandIntro</textarea>
                </div>

                <div class="mb-4 text-start">
                    <label class="form-label fw-semibold">創作起始日</label>
                    <input class="form-control" value="@(Model.StartDate?.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd") ?? "")" readonly />
                </div>

                <hr class="my-4" />

                <!-- ===== 必填 3 欄 ===== -->
                <!-- 1) 品牌圖片（必填 + 預覽） -->
                <div class="mb-4 text-start">
                    <label class="form-label fw-semibold">品牌圖片（必填）</label>

                    <input asp-for="BrandImageFile"
                           id="BrandImageFile"
                           type="file"
                           accept="image/*"
                           class="form-control"
                           required />

                    <div class="mt-2">
                        <img id="BrandImagePreview"
                             class="img-fluid rounded shadow-sm d-none"
                             style="max-height:220px; object-fit:cover;" />
                    </div>

                    <span class="text-danger" asp-validation-for="BrandImageFile"></span>
                </div>

                <!-- 2) 銀行代碼 -->
                <div class="mb-3 text-start">
                    <label class="form-label fw-semibold">銀行代碼（必填）</label>
                    <input asp-for="BankCode" class="form-control" placeholder="例如：004" />
                    <span class="text-danger" asp-validation-for="BankCode"></span>
                </div>

                <!-- 3) 銀行帳號 -->
                <div class="mb-4 text-start">
                    <label class="form-label fw-semibold">銀行帳號（必填）</label>
                    <input asp-for="BankAccount" class="form-control" placeholder="請輸入帳號" />
                    <span class="text-danger" asp-validation-for="BankAccount"></span>
                </div>

                <div class="d-flex gap-2 justify-content-end">
                    <a asp-controller="Member" asp-action="Index" class="btn btn-outline-secondary">
                        取消
                    </a>
                    <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary">
                        確認送出
                    </button>
                </div>
            </form>

        </div>
    </div>
</div>

@section Scripts {
    <partial name="_ValidationScriptsPartial" />
    <script>
        (function () {
            const input = document.getElementById("BrandImageFile");
            const preview = document.getElementById("BrandImagePreview");
            if (!input || !preview) return;

            input.addEventListener("change", function () {
                const file = input.files && input.files[0];
                if (!file) {
                    preview.src = "";
                    preview.classList.add("d-none");
                    return;
                }
                const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
                preview.src = url;
                preview.classList.remove("d-none");
            });
        })();
    </script>
}

                    <textarea asp-for="BrandIntro"
                              class="form-control"
                              rows="5"
                              placeholder="寫一些你的創作理念 / 風格 / 經歷..."></textarea>

@section Styles {
    <style>
        .brand-upload {
            border: 1px dashed #d0d7de;
            border-radius: 12px;
            padding: 16px;
            background: #fafafa;
        }

        .brand-preview {
            width: 160px;
            height: 160px;
            border-radius: 16px;
            object-fit: cover;
            background: #fff;
            border: 1px solid rgba(0,0,0,.08);
        }
    </style>
}

@section Scripts {
    <partial name="_ValidationScriptsPartial" />

    <script>
        (function () {
            const input = document.getElementById("brandImageInput");
            const img = document.getElementById("brandPreview");

            if (!input || !img) return;

            input.addEventListener("change", function () {
                const file = input.files && input.files[0];
                if (!file) return;

                const reader = new FileReader();
                reader.onload = e => {
                    img.src = e.target.result;
                };
                reader.readAsDataURL(file);
            });
        })();
    </script>
}

@model CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.Creator.VMCreatorIndex

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "創作者";
}

<div class="container py-5" style="max-width:1200px;">

    <!-- 標題 -->
    <div class="text-center mb-2">
        <h2 class="fw-bold">探索創作者</h2>
        <p class="text-muted">發掘最新加入的創作者</p>
    </div>

    <!-- 搜尋 -->
    <form method="get" class="row justify-content-center mb-3">
        <div class="col-md-6 d-flex gap-2">
            <input type="text"
                   name="keyword"
                   value="@Model.Query.Keyword"
                   class="form-control"
                   placeholder="搜尋創作者名稱或介紹..." />

            <button type="submit"
                    class="btn btn-dark text-nowrap px-4">
                <i class="bi bi-search me-1"></i>搜尋
            </button>
        </div>
    </form>

    @if (!Model.Creators.Any())
    {
        <div class="text-center text-muted py-5">
            尚無符合條件的創作者
        </div>
    }
    else
    {
        <div class="row g-4">

            @foreach (var creator in Model.Creators)
            {
                <div class="col-md-6 col-lg-4">

                    <div class="card shadow-sm border-0 h-100 p-card">

                        <!-- 整張卡片超連結 -->
                        <a asp-action="Profile"
                           asp-route-id="@creator.CreatorID"
                           class="text-decoration-none text-dark d-block h-100">

                            <div class="card-body text-center d-flex flex-column">

                                <div class="d-flex justify-content-center mb-3">
                                    <img src="@creator.ImagePath"
                                         class="rounded-circle"
                                         style="width:100px;height:100px;object-fit:cover;" />
                                </div>

                                <div class="fw-semibold h5 mb-2 text-truncate">
                                    <i class="bi bi-filter-right"></i>
                                    @creator.BrandName
                                    <i class="bi bi-filter-left"></i>
                                </div>

                                <div class="text-muted small p-preview mb-2">
                                    @creator.BrandIntro
                                </div>

                                <div class="text-muted small text-end mt-auto w-100">
                                    <i class="bi bi-people"></i>
                                    @creator.FollowerCount 位粉絲

                                    <span class="mx-2">•</span>

                                    @(creator.CreatedAt.ToLocalTime().ToString("yyyy/MM/dd"))
                                </div>

                            </div>
                        </a>

                    </div>

                </div>
            }

        </div>
    }
    @if (Model.TotalPages > 1)
    {
        <nav class="mt-5">
            <ul class="pagination justify-content-center">

                @if (Model.HasPreviousPage)
                {
                    <li class="page-item">
                        <a class="page-link"
                           asp-action="Index"
                           asp-route-page="@(Model.Query.Page - 1)"
                           asp-route-keyword="@Model.Query.Keyword">
                            上一頁
                        </a>
                    </li>
                }

                @for (int i = 1; i <= Model.TotalPages; i++)
                {
                    <li class="page-item @(i == Model.Query.Page ? "active" : "")">
                        <a class="page-link"
                           asp-action="Index"
                           asp-route-page="@i"
                           asp-route-keyword="@Model.Query.Keyword">
                            @i
                        </a>
                    </li>
                }

                @if (Model.HasNextPage)
                {
                    <li class="page-item">
                        <a class="page-link"
                           asp-action="Index"
                           asp-route-page="@(Model.Query.Page + 1)"
                           asp-route-keyword="@Model.Query.Keyword">
                            下一頁
                        </a>
                    </li>
                }

            </ul>
        </nav>
    }

</div>

FILE: CraftDailyCorner\Views\CreatorOrders\Index.cshtml

@using CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.CreatorOrder
@model VMCreatorOrderList

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "訂單管理";
    var currentStatus = Model.StatusFilter?.ToLower() ?? "new";
    var isNewPage = currentStatus == "new";
}

<div class="container py-4">

    <!-- 標題區 -->
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
        <div>
            <h2 class="fw-bold mb-1">訂單管理</h2>
            <p class="text-muted mb-0">管理您的訂單流程</p>
        </div>

        <div class="d-flex gap-2">
            <a asp-controller="Creator"
               asp-action="Dashboard"
               class="btn btn-outline-secondary">
                回創作者中心
            </a>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- 狀態切換 Tabs -->
    <ul class="nav nav-tabs mb-4">
        <li class="nav-item">
            <a class="nav-link @(currentStatus == "new" ? "active" : "")"
               asp-action="Index"
               asp-route-status="new">
                新訂單
            </a>
        </li>
        <li class="nav-item">
            <a class="nav-link @(currentStatus == "processing" ? "active" : "")"
               asp-action="Index"
               asp-route-status="processing">
                準備中
            </a>
        </li>
        <li class="nav-item">
            <a class="nav-link @(currentStatus == "shipping" ? "active" : "")"
               asp-action="Index"
               asp-route-status="shipping">
                配送中
            </a>
        </li>
        <li class="nav-item">
            <a class="nav-link @(currentStatus == "history" ? "active" : "")"
               asp-action="Index"
               asp-route-status="history">
                歷史訂單
            </a>
        </li>
    </ul>

    <!-- 訂單列表 -->
    @if (!Model.Orders.Any())
    {
        <div class="alert alert-info">
            目前沒有訂單。
        </div>
    }
    else
    {
        <!-- 新訂單才需要包 Form -->
        <form asp-action="BatchPrint" method="post">

            <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
                <div class="table-responsive">
                    <table class="table table-hover align-middle mb-0">
                        <thead class="table-light">
                            <tr>
                                @if (isNewPage)
                                {
                                    <th style="width:50px;">
                                        <input type="checkbox" id="checkAll" />
                                    </th>
                                }
                                <th>訂單編號</th>
                                <th>收件人</th>
                                <th>建立時間</th>
                                <th>金額</th>
                                <th>狀態</th>
                                <th class="text-end">操作</th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>

                        <tbody>
                            @foreach (var order in Model.Orders)
                            {
                                <tr>
                                    @if (isNewPage)
                                    {
                                        <td>
                                            <input type="checkbox"
                                                   name="SelectedOrderIDs"
                                                   value="@order.OrderID"
                                                   class="order-checkbox" />
                                        </td>
                                    }

                                    <td class="fw-semibold">@order.OrderID</td>
                                    <td>@order.ReceiverName</td>
                                    <td>@order.CreatedAt.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm")</td>
                                    <td>@order.TotalAmount.ToString("N0")</td>

                                    <td>
                                        @switch (order.StatusID)
                                        {
                                            case 2:
                                                <span class="badge bg-danger">已付款</span>
                                                break;
                                            case 3:
                                                <span class="badge bg-warning text-dark">準備中</span>
                                                break;
                                            case 4:
                                                <span class="badge bg-primary">配送中</span>
                                                @if (order.HasShipment && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(order.TrackingNo))
                                                {
                                                    <span class="small text-muted ms-2">
                                                        (物流編號：@order.TrackingNo)
                                                    </span>
                                                }
                                                break;
                                            case 5:
                                                <span class="badge bg-success">完成</span>
                                                break;
                                            case 6:
                                                <span class="badge bg-secondary">取消</span>
                                                break;
                                            default:
                                                <span class="badge bg-light text-dark">@order.StatusName</span>
                                                break;
                                        }
                                    </td>

                                    <td class="text-end">
                                        <a asp-action="Detail"
                                           asp-route-id="@order.OrderID"
                                           class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary">
                                            查看
                                        </a>

                                        @if (order.CanProcess && !isNewPage)
                                        {
                                            <form asp-action="StartProcessing"
                                                  method="post"
                                                  class="d-inline">
                                                <input type="hidden" name="id" value="@order.OrderID" />
                                                <button type="submit"
                                                        class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-warning ms-2">
                                                    開始處理
                                                </button>
                                            </form>
                                        }
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            }
                        </tbody>

                    </table>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- 批次列印按鈕（只在新訂單） -->
            @if (isNewPage)
            {
                <div class="mt-3 text-end">
                    <button type="submit" class="btn btn-danger">
                        <i class="bi bi-printer"></i> 列印撿貨單
                    </button>
                </div>
            }
        </form>
    }

    <!-- 分頁 -->
    @if (Model.TotalPages > 1)
    {
        <nav class="mt-4">
            <ul class="pagination justify-content-center">
                @for (int i = 1; i <= Model.TotalPages; i++)
                {
                    <li class="page-item @(i == Model.CurrentPage ? "active" : "")">
                        <a class="page-link"
                           asp-action="Index"
                           asp-route-status="@Model.StatusFilter"
                           asp-route-page="@i">
                            @i
                        </a>
                    </li>
                }
            </ul>
        </nav>
    }

</div>

@section Scripts {
    <script>
        const checkAll = document.getElementById('checkAll');

        if (checkAll) {
            checkAll.addEventListener('change', function () {
                document.querySelectorAll('.order-checkbox')
                    .forEach(cb => cb.checked = this.checked);
            });
        }
    </script>
}

    @using CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.CreatorProduct
    @model VMCreatorProductForm

    @{
        ViewData["Title"] = "新增商品";
    }

    <div class="container py-5" style="max-width:1100px;">

        <h2 class="fw-bold mb-4">新增商品</h2>

        <form asp-action="Create"
              method="post"
              enctype="multipart/form-data">

            @Html.AntiForgeryToken()

            <div asp-validation-summary="ModelOnly"
                 class="text-danger mb-3"></div>

            <!-- ================= 基本資料 ================= -->

            <div class="card shadow-sm border-0 mb-4">
                <div class="card-body">

                    <div class="mb-3">
                        <label asp-for="ProductName" class="form-label fw-semibold"></label>
                        <input asp-for="ProductName" class="form-control" />
                        <span asp-validation-for="ProductName" class="text-danger"></span>
                    </div>

                    <div class="mb-3">
                        <label asp-for="Description" class="form-label fw-semibold"></label>
                        <textarea asp-for="Description"
                                  class="form-control"
                                  rows="4"></textarea>
                        <span asp-validation-for="Description" class="text-danger"></span>
                    </div>

                    <div class="mb-3">
                        <label asp-for="Price" class="form-label fw-semibold"></label>
                        <input asp-for="Price"
                               type="number"
                               step="0.01"
                               class="form-control" />
                        <span asp-validation-for="Price" class="text-danger"></span>
                    </div>

                    <div class="mb-3">
                        <label asp-for="StatusID" class="form-label fw-semibold"></label>
                        <select asp-for="StatusID"
                                asp-items="Model.StatusSelectList"
                                class="form-select">
                        </select>
                    </div>

                </div>
            </div>


            <!-- ================= 商品圖片 ================= -->

            <div class="card shadow-sm border-0 mb-4">
                <div class="card-header bg-light fw-semibold">
                    商品圖片（最多 10 張）
                </div>

                <div class="card-body">

                    <input asp-for="ImageFiles"
                           type="file"
                           id="image-input"
                           class="form-control"
                           accept="image/*"
                           multiple />

                    <div class="form-text mb-3">
                        建議尺寸 800x800，PNG / JPG

                                {
                                    <div class="col-md-3 mb-2">

                        @foreach (var tag in Model.TagSelectList)
                        {
                            <div class="col-md-3 mb-2">

                                           type="checkbox"

                                           value="@tag.Value"
                                       @(Model.SelectedTagIds.Contains(int.Parse(tag.Value)) ? "checked" : "") />

                    建立商品

 @section Scripts {
    <partial name="_ValidationScriptsPartial" />
        <script>

            document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {

                const imageInput = document.getElementById("image-input");
                const previewContainer = document.getElementById("preview-container");
                const form = document.querySelector("form");
               const maxFiles = 10;

                // ===== 多圖預覽 =====
                imageInput.addEventListener("change", function () {

                    previewContainer.innerHTML = "";

                   if (this.files.length > maxFiles) {
                       alert(`最多只能上傳 ${maxFiles} 張圖片`);
                        this.value = "";
                        return;
                    }

                    Array.from(this.files).forEach(file => {

                        const reader = new FileReader();

                        reader.onload = function (e) {

                            const col = document.createElement("div");
                            col.className = "col-md-3 mb-3";

                            col.innerHTML = `
                                <img src="${e.target.result}"
                                     class="img-fluid rounded shadow-sm"
                                     style="height:160px;object-fit:cover;" />
                            `;

                            previewContainer.appendChild(col);
                        };

                        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                    });
                });

                // ===== 分類限制 =====
                document.querySelectorAll(".category-checkbox")
                    .forEach(cb => {
                        cb.addEventListener("change", function () {

                            const checked =
                                document.querySelectorAll(".category-checkbox:checked");

                            if (checked.length > 3) {
                                this.checked = false;
                                alert("商品分類最多只能選 3 個");
                            }
                        });
                    });

                // ===== 標籤限制 =====
                document.querySelectorAll(".tag-checkbox")
                    .forEach(cb => {
                        cb.addEventListener("change", function () {

                            const checked =
                                document.querySelectorAll(".tag-checkbox:checked");

                            if (checked.length > 10) {
                                this.checked = false;
                                alert("商品標籤最多只能選 10 個");
                            }
                        });
                    });

            // ===== 前端驗證 =====
            form.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {

                let hasError = false;
                // ===== 先清空舊錯誤 =====
                const imageError = document.querySelector("[data-valmsg-for='ImageFiles']");
                const categoryError = document.querySelector("[data-valmsg-for='SelectedCategoryIds']");
                const stockError = document.querySelector("[data-valmsg-for='StockQty']");

                imageError.textContent = "";
                imageError.classList.remove("field-validation-error");
                imageError.classList.add("field-validation-valid");

                categoryError.textContent = "";
                categoryError.classList.remove("field-validation-error");
                categoryError.classList.add("field-validation-valid");

                stockError.textContent = "";
                stockError.classList.remove("field-validation-error");
                stockError.classList.add("field-validation-valid");

                // ===== 圖片 =====
                if (!imageInput.files || imageInput.files.length === 0) {
                    document.querySelector("[data-valmsg-for='ImageFiles']")
                        .textContent = "請至少上傳 1 張商品圖片";
                    hasError = true;
                }

                // ===== 分類 =====
                const selectedCategories =
                    document.querySelectorAll(".category-checkbox:checked");

                if (selectedCategories.length === 0) {
                    document.querySelector("[data-valmsg-for='SelectedCategoryIds']")
                        .textContent = "請至少選擇 1 個商品分類";
                    hasError = true;
                }

                // ===== 庫存 =====
                const stockInput = document.querySelector("[name='StockQty']");

                if (parseInt(stockInput.value) <= 0 || !stockInput.value) {
                    document.querySelector("[data-valmsg-for='StockQty']")
                        .textContent = "庫存必須大於 0";
                    hasError = true;
                }

                if (hasError) e.preventDefault();
            });
         });
    </script>
}

        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", async function () {

            // ===== 載入圖片管理 Partial =====

            const response = await fetch(
                `/ImageManagement/ProductPartial/${productId}`
            );

            const html = await response.text();
            document.getElementById("imageManagementContainer").innerHTML = html;

            if (typeof initImageManagement === "function") {
                initImageManagement();
            }

            // ===== 分類限制 =====
            document.querySelectorAll(".category-checkbox")
                .forEach(cb => {
                    cb.addEventListener("change", function () {
                        const checked =
                            document.querySelectorAll(".category-checkbox:checked");

                    });
                });

            // ===== 標籤限制 =====

                .forEach(cb => {

                        const checked =
                            document.querySelectorAll(".tag-checkbox:checked");

                        if (checked.length > 10) {
                            this.checked = false;
                            alert("商品標籤最多只能選 10 個");
                        }
                    });
                });

        });
    </script>

FILE: CraftDailyCorner\Views\CreatorProducts\Index.cshtml

@model CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.CreatorProduct.VMCreatorProductList

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "商品管理";
}

<div class="container py-4">

    <!-- 標題區 -->
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
        <div>
            <h2 class="fw-bold mb-1">商品管理</h2>
            <p class="text-muted mb-0">管理您的創作結晶</p>
        </div>

        <div class="d-flex gap-2">
            <a asp-controller="Creator"
               asp-action="Dashboard"
               class="btn btn-outline-secondary">
                回創作者中心
            </a>

            <a asp-action="Create" class="btn btn-primary">
                <i class="bi bi-plus-circle"></i> 新增商品
            </a>
        </div>
    </div>

    @if (!Model.Products.Any())
    {
        <div class="alert alert-info">
            目前尚未建立任何商品。
        </div>
    }
    else
    {
        <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
            <div class="table-responsive">
                <table class="table align-middle mb-0">

                    <thead class="table-light">
                        <tr>
                            <th>商品名稱</th>
                            <th>價格</th>
                            <th>狀態</th>
                            <th>商品狀態</th>
                            <th>建立時間</th>
                            <th class="text-end">操作</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>

                    <tbody>
                        @foreach (var item in Model.Products)
                        {
                            <tr>
                                <td>
                                    <div class="d-flex align-items-center gap-3">
                                        <img src="@(string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.CoverImageUrl)
                                                    ? "/Photos/04ProductImage/default.png" 
                                                    : $"/Photos/04ProductImage/Medium/{item.CoverImageUrl}.png")"
                                             style="width:60px;height:60px;object-fit:cover;"
                                             class="rounded border" />

                                        <div class="fw-semibold">
                                            <a asp-controller="Products" asp-action="Detail" asp-route-id="@item.ProductID"
                                               class="text-decoration-none">
                                                @item.ProductName
                                            </a>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </td>

                                <td>
                                    @item.Price.ToString("N0")
                                </td>

                                <td>
                                    @if (item.StatusName == "上架中")
                                    {
                                        <span class="badge bg-success">上架中</span>
                                    }
                                    else if (item.StatusName == "草稿")
                                    {
                                        <span class="badge bg-primary">草稿</span>
                                    }
                                    else
                                    {
                                        <span class="badge bg-danger">下架</span>
                                    }
                                </td>

                                <td>
                                    @if (item.IsOutOfStock)
                                    {
                                        <span class="badge bg-danger">缺貨</span>
                                    }
                                    else if (item.IsLowStock)
                                    {
                                        <span class="badge bg-warning text-dark">低庫存 (@item.StockQty)</span>
                                    }
                                    else
                                    {
                                        <span class="badge bg-success">正常 (@item.StockQty)</span>
                                    }
                                </td>

                                <td>
                                    @item.CreatedAt.ToLocalTime().ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm")
                                </td>

                                <td class="text-end">

                                    <a asp-action="Edit"
                                       asp-route-id="@item.ProductID"
                                       class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary me-2">
                                        編輯
                                    </a>

                                    <form asp-action="Delete"
                                          asp-route-id="@item.ProductID"
                                          method="post"
                                          class="d-inline"
                                          onsubmit="return confirm('確定要下架此商品嗎？');">

                                        <button type="submit"
                                                class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger">
                                            下架
                                        </button>
                                    </form>

                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        }
                    </tbody>

                </table>
            </div>
        </div>
    }

</div>

                        </p>

                           class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary mt-2">

                        </a>

                        </a>
                    </div>

                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 右側：申請成為創作者（col-4） -->
        <div class="col-md-4">
            <div class="card hover-card h-100 shadow-sm">
                <div class="card-body text-center d-flex flex-column justify-content-center">

                    @* ===== 1. 已是創作者 ===== *@
                    @if (Model.IsCreator)
                    {
                        <div class="mb-2">
                            <i class="bi bi-check-circle fs-1 text-success"></i>
                        </div>
                        <div class="fs-4 fw-semibold mb-1">你已是創作者</div>
                        <p class="text-muted small mb-3">可以開始上架作品，分享你的創作</p>

                        <a asp-controller="Creator"
                           asp-action="Dashboard"
                           class="btn btn-success btn-sm fs-5">
                            前往創作者專區
                        </a>
                    }
                    @* ===== 2. 申請審核中 (Pending) ===== *@
                    else if (Model.CreatorApplicationStatusCode == "Pending")
                    {
                        <div class="mb-2">
                            <i class="bi bi-hourglass-split fs-1 text-warning"></i>
                        </div>
                        <div class="fs-4 fw-semibold mb-1">申請審核中</div>
                        <p class="text-muted small mb-3">我們正在審核你的申請，請耐心等候</p>

                        <button class="btn btn-outline-secondary btn-sm fs-5" disabled>
                            審核中
                        </button>
                    }
                    @* ===== 已通過 (Approved) 且還不是創作者：去確認頁 ===== *@
                    else if (Model.CreatorApplicationStatusCode == "Approved")
                    {
                        <div class="mb-2">
                            <i class="bi bi-award fs-1 text-success"></i>
                        </div>
                        <div class="fs-4 fw-semibold mb-1">恭喜審核通過！</div>
                        <p class="text-muted small mb-3">完成最後資料填寫即可開通創作者功能</p>

                        <a asp-controller="Creator"
                           asp-action="ApprovedConfirm"
                           asp-route-applicationId="@Model.CreatorApplicationId"
                           class="btn btn-success btn-sm fs-5">
                            前往確認
                        </a>
                    }
                    @* ===== 已拒絕 (Rejected)：去拒絕確認頁 ===== *@
                    else if (Model.CreatorApplicationStatusCode == "Rejected")
                    {
                        <div class="mb-2">
                            <i class="bi bi-emoji-frown fs-1 text-secondary"></i>
                        </div>
                        <div class="fs-4 fw-semibold mb-1">很可惜，未通過審核</div>
                        <p class="text-muted small mb-3">您可以先查看原因並確認結果</p>

                        <a asp-controller="Creator"
                           asp-action="RejectedConfirm"
                           asp-route-applicationId="@Model.CreatorApplicationId"
                           class="btn btn-outline-secondary btn-sm fs-5">
                            查看結果
                        </a>
                    }
                    @* =====  尚未申請  ===== *@
                    else
                    {
                        <div class="stat-icon-wrapper mb-2">
                            <i class="bi bi-stars fs-1 text-warning"></i>
                        </div>
                        <div class="fs-4 fw-semibold mb-1">成為創作者</div>
                        <p class="text-muted small mb-3">上架作品、分享創作，讓更多人看見你的創意</p>

                        <a asp-controller="Creator"
                           asp-action="Apply"
                           class="btn text-white rounded-pill btn-sm fs-5"
                           style="background-color: var(--bs-primary);">

                        </a>
                    }

            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

@using CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.Member
@model VMEditProfile

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "個人資料";

    string avatarUrl = string.IsNullOrEmpty(Model.ImageUrl)
        ? "/Photos/01Member/Medium/default.png"
        : $"/Photos/01Member/Medium/{Model.ImageUrl}.png";
}

<div class="container my-5">

    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
        <h3 class="fw-bold">個人資料</h3>

        <a asp-action="Index"
           class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary">
            返回會員中心
        </a>
    </div>

    @if (TempData["Success"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-success">
            @TempData["Success"]
        </div>
    }

    <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
        <div class="card-body">

            <form asp-action="Profile"
                  method="post"
                  enctype="multipart/form-data">

                @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                <input type="hidden" asp-for="MemberID" />
                <input type="hidden" asp-for="ImageUrl" />

                <!-- 頭像（可即時預覽） -->
                <div class="mb-4 text-center">

                    <img id="avatarPreview"
                         src="@avatarUrl"
                         class="rounded-circle border mb-3"
                         style="width:180px;height:180px;object-fit:cover;" />

                    <div class="d-flex flex-column align-items-center gap-1">
                        <input asp-for="AvatarFile"
                               type="file"
                               class="form-control"
                               accept="image/png,image/jpeg"
                               onchange="previewImage({input: this,previewSelector: '#avatarPreview'})"
                               style="max-width:280px;" />

                        <small class="text-muted">
                            建議比例 1:1，支援 JPG / PNG
                        </small>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!--  顯示名稱  -->
                <div class="mb-3">
                    <label asp-for="DisplayName" class="form-label"></label>
                    <input asp-for="DisplayName" class="form-control" />
                    <span asp-validation-for="DisplayName"
                          class="text-danger"></span>
                </div>
                <!--電子郵件不可修改-->
                <div class="mb-3">
                    <label asp-for="Email" class="form-label"></label>
                    <input asp-for="Email" class="form-control" value="@Model.Email" readonly />
                    <small class="text-muted">
                        ※ 電子郵件無法修改，如需變更請聯絡客服
                    </small>

@model CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.Message.VMMessageIndex

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "我的訊息";
}
@section Styles {
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="~/css/chat.css" asp-append-version="true" />
}
<div class="container-fluid py-4">

    @if (TempData["Warning"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-warning shadow-sm border-0 rounded-3">
            <i class="bi bi-exclamation-triangle-fill me-2"></i> @TempData["Warning"]
        </div>
    }

    <div class="row g-3 chat-container">

        <div class="col-lg-4 h-100">
            <div class="card shadow-sm border-0 h-100 rounded-4 overflow-hidden">
                <div class="card-body p-0 d-flex flex-column h-100">

                    <div class="p-3 border-bottom bg-white z-1">
                        <h5 class="fw-bold mb-1">訊息</h5>
                        <div class="text-muted small">選擇一個對話查看內容</div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="flex-grow-1 overflow-auto chat-scroll bg-white">
                        @if (Model.Conversations == null || !Model.Conversations.Any())
                        {
                            <div class="p-5 text-center text-muted h-100 d-flex flex-column justify-content-center">
                                <i class="bi bi-chat-dots fs-1 d-block mb-3 text-light"></i>
                                目前沒有對話
                            </div>
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            <div class="list-group list-group-flush border-0">
                                @foreach (var item in Model.Conversations)
                                {
                                    // 改用自訂的 chat-active，避免 Bootstrap 預設 active 導致文字變白
                                    var activeClass = item.IsActive ? "chat-active" : "";
                                    var timeText = item.LastMessageAt.Date == DateTime.Today
                                    ? item.LastMessageAt.ToString("HH:mm")
                                    : item.LastMessageAt.ToString("MM/dd");

                                    <a asp-controller="Messages"
                                       asp-action="Index"
                                       asp-route-threadId="@item.ThreadID"
                                       class="list-group-item list-group-item-action border-bottom-0 border-top-0 border-end-0 py-3 @activeClass">

                                        <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-start mb-1">
                                            <div class="fw-bold text-truncate pe-2 text-dark">
                                                @item.DisplayName
                                            </div>
                                            <small class="text-nowrap text-muted">
                                                @timeText
                                            </small>
                                        </div>

                                        @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(item.ProductName))
                                        {
                                            <div class="small text-muted mb-2 d-flex align-items-center">
                                                <i class="bi bi-box-seam me-1"></i> @item.ProductName
                                            </div>
                                        }

                                        <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">
                                            <div class="small text-truncate pe-2 text-secondary">
                                                @(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(item.LastMessagePreview) ? "尚無訊息" : item.LastMessagePreview)
                                            </div>

                                            @if (item.UnreadCount > 0)
                                            {
                                                <span class="badge bg-danger rounded-pill px-2 shadow-sm">@item.UnreadCount</span>
                                            }
                                        </div>
                                    </a>
                                }
                            </div>
                        }
                    </div>

                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="col-lg-8 h-100">
            <div class="card shadow-sm border-0 h-100 rounded-4 overflow-hidden">
                <div class="card-body p-0 d-flex flex-column h-100">

                    @if (Model.CurrentThread == null)
                    {
                        <div class="flex-grow-1 d-flex flex-column justify-content-center align-items-center text-muted p-4 bg-light">
                            <div class="bg-white p-4 rounded-circle shadow-sm mb-3">
                                <i class="bi bi-chat-square-text fs-1 text-primary"></i>
                            </div>
                            <h5 class="fw-bold text-dark">請先選擇一個對話</h5>
                            <div class="small mt-1 text-secondary">左側選擇對話後，即可查看聊天內容</div>
                        </div>
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        <div class="p-3 border-bottom bg-white z-1 shadow-sm">
                            <div class="fw-bold fs-5">@Model.CurrentThread.DisplayName</div>

                            @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Model.CurrentThread.SubTitle))
                            {
                                <div class="text-muted small">@Model.CurrentThread.SubTitle</div>
                            }
                        </div>

                        @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Model.CurrentThread.ProductID))
                        {
                            <div class="p-3 border-bottom bg-white">
                                <div class="card border-0 shadow-sm bg-light rounded-3">
                                    <div class="card-body p-3">
                                        <div class="small text-primary fw-semibold mb-2">
                                            <i class="bi bi-info-circle me-1"></i> 正在詢問商品
                                        </div>

                                        <div class="d-flex align-items-center gap-3">
                                            <div class="flex-shrink-0">
                                                @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Model.CurrentThread.ProductImageUrl))
