                                                         alt="商品圖片"
                                                         class="rounded border border-white shadow-sm"
                                                         style="width:80px;height:80px;object-fit:cover;" />
                                                }
                                                else
                                                {
                                                    <div class="rounded border d-flex align-items-center justify-content-center bg-white text-muted shadow-sm"
                                                         style="width:80px;height:80px;">
                                                        <i class="bi bi-image fs-3 text-light"></i>
                                                    </div>
                                                }
                                            </div>

                                            <div class="flex-grow-1">
                                                <div class="fw-bold text-dark">@Model.CurrentThread.ProductName</div>

                                                @if (Model.CurrentThread.ProductPrice.HasValue)
                                                {
                                                    <div class="text-danger fw-semibold mt-1">
                                                        NT$ @Model.CurrentThread.ProductPrice.Value.ToString("N0")
                                                    </div>
                                                }

                                                <a asp-controller="Products"
                                                   asp-action="Detail"
                                                   asp-route-id="@Model.CurrentThread.ProductID"
                                                   class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary rounded-pill px-3 mt-2">
                                                    查看商品
                                                </a>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        }

                        @if (Model.CurrentThread.QuickReplyTemplates != null && Model.CurrentThread.QuickReplyTemplates.Any())
                        {
                            <div class="p-2 border-bottom bg-white">
                                <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center px-2 py-1">
                                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-link text-decoration-none text-muted p-0"
                                            type="button"
                                            data-bs-toggle="collapse"
                                            data-bs-target="#quickReplyCollapse"
                                            aria-expanded="true"
                                            aria-controls="quickReplyCollapse">
                                        <i class="bi bi-lightning-charge-fill text-warning"></i> 展開快速回覆
                                    </button>

                                    <a asp-controller="Messages"
                                       asp-action="MessageTemplates"
                                       class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary rounded-pill" style="font-size:0.75rem;">
                                        <i class="bi bi-gear"></i> 管理
                                    </a>
                                </div>

                                <div class="collapse show mt-2 px-2 pb-2" id="quickReplyCollapse">
                                    <div class="d-flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                                        @foreach (var template in Model.CurrentThread.QuickReplyTemplates)
                                        {
                                            <button type="button"
                                                    class="btn btn-sm btn-light border rounded-pill text-secondary quick-reply-btn hover-shadow"
                                                    data-content="@template.Content">
                                                @template.Title
                                            </button>
                                        }
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        }
                        else if (Model.IsCreatorSide)
                        {
                            <div class="p-3 border-bottom bg-white d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">
                                <div class="small text-muted"><i class="bi bi-info-circle me-1"></i> 尚無快速回覆模板</div>
                                <a asp-controller="Messages"
                                   asp-action="QuickReplyTemplates"
                                   class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary rounded-pill">
                                    新增模板
                                </a>
                            </div>
                        }

                        <div id="messageScroll" class="flex-grow-1 p-4 overflow-auto chat-scroll" style="background-color:#f8faff;">

                            @if (!Model.CurrentThread.Messages.Any())
                            {
                                <div class="text-center mt-5">
                                    <div class="d-inline-block p-3 rounded-circle bg-white shadow-sm mb-3">
                                        <i class="bi bi-emoji-smile fs-2 text-muted"></i>
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="text-muted fw-semibold">尚無訊息，開始第一則對話吧！</div>
                                </div>
                            }
                            else
                            {
                                @foreach (var msg in Model.CurrentThread.Messages)
                                {
                                    <div class="d-flex mb-3 @(msg.IsMine ? "justify-content-end" : "justify-content-start")">

                                        <div class="msg-bubble @(msg.IsMine ? "msg-mine" : "msg-other")">

                                            <div style="white-space: pre-wrap;">@msg.Content</div>

                                            <div class="msg-time @(msg.IsMine ? "text-white-50 text-end" : "text-secondary text-start")">
                                                @msg.CreatedAt.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm")
                                            </div>

                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                }
                            }
                        </div>

                        <div class="p-3 chat-input-area z-1">
                            <form asp-controller="Messages"
                                  asp-action="Send"
                                  method="post"
                                  class="d-flex flex-column gap-2">
                                @Html.AntiForgeryToken()

                                <input type="hidden" name="threadId" value="@Model.CurrentThread.ThreadID" />

                                <textarea id="messageTextarea"
                                      name="content"
                                      class="form-control chat-textarea px-3 py-2"
                                      rows="2"
                                      placeholder="輸入訊息..."
                                      required>@Model.CurrentThread.DraftContent</textarea>

                                <div class="d-flex justify-content-end align-items-center mt-1">
                                    <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary rounded-pill px-4 fw-semibold shadow-sm">
                                        送出 <i class="bi bi-send-fill ms-1"></i>
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </form>
                        </div>
                    }

                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

    </div>
</div>

@section Scripts {
    <script>
        // 快速回覆按鈕邏輯保留
        document.querySelectorAll(".quick-reply-btn").forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener("click", function () {
                const textarea = document.getElementById("messageTextarea");
                if (!textarea) return;

                const content = this.dataset.content || "";

                // 附加到現有內容後方並自動換行，或直接覆蓋 (依照原本邏輯為直接覆寫)
                textarea.value = content;
                textarea.focus();
            });
        });

        // 捲軸自動置底邏輯保留
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
            const container = document.getElementById("messageScroll");
            if (container) {
                container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;
            }
        });
    </script>
}

FILE: CraftDailyCorner\Views\Messages\MessageTemplates.cshtml
==================================================

@model CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.Message.VMMessageTemplateManage
@using CraftDailyCorner.Models.enums;

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "訊息模板管理";

    var autoReplyTemplate = Model.Items
        .FirstOrDefault(x => x.TriggerType == (byte)AutoReplyTemplateTriggerType.FirstMessage);

    var quickReplyTemplates = Model.Items
        .Where(x => x.TriggerType == (byte)AutoReplyTemplateTriggerType.QuickReply)
        .ToList();
}

<div class="container py-4">

    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
        <div>
            <h2 class="fw-bold mb-1">訊息模板管理</h2>
            <p class="text-muted mb-0">管理自動回覆與快速回覆模板</p>
        </div>

        <div class="d-flex gap-2">
            <a asp-controller="Messages"
               asp-action="Index"
               class="btn btn-outline-secondary">
                返回我的訊息
            </a>

            <a asp-controller="Messages"
               asp-action="CreateMessageTemplate"
               class="btn btn-primary">
                <i class="bi bi-plus-circle me-1"></i> 新增快速回覆模板
            </a>
        </div>
    </div>

    @if (TempData["Success"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-success">@TempData["Success"]</div>
    }

    @if (TempData["Warning"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-warning">@TempData["Warning"]</div>
    }

    <!-- ============================= -->
    <!-- 第一次訊息自動回覆（固定最上面） -->
    <!-- ============================= -->
    <div class="card shadow-sm border-0 mb-4">
        <div class="card-header bg-white">
            <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">
                <div>
                    <div class="fw-bold">第一次訊息自動回覆</div>
                    <div class="small text-muted">每位創作者只有 1 個，只能編輯、啟用 / 禁用，不能新增</div>
                </div>

                @if (autoReplyTemplate != null)
                {
                    @if (autoReplyTemplate.IsActive)
                    {
                        <span class="badge bg-success">啟用中</span>
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        <span class="badge bg-secondary">已禁用</span>
                    }
                }
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="card-body">
            @if (autoReplyTemplate == null)
            {
                <div class="alert alert-warning mb-0">
                    尚未建立第一次訊息自動回覆模板。<br />
                    請檢查創作者確認流程是否有建立預設模板。
                </div>
            }
            else
            {
                <div class="border rounded p-3">
                    <div class="fw-semibold mb-2">@autoReplyTemplate.Title</div>

                    <div class="text-muted small mb-2">
                        類型：@autoReplyTemplate.TriggerTypeName
                    </div>

                    <div class="mb-3" style="white-space: pre-wrap;">@autoReplyTemplate.Content</div>

                    <div class="d-flex gap-2">
                        <a asp-controller="Messages"
                           asp-action="EditMessageTemplate"
                           asp-route-id="@autoReplyTemplate.TemplateID"
                           class="btn btn-outline-primary btn-sm">
                            編輯
                        </a>

                        @if (autoReplyTemplate.IsActive)
                        {
                            <form asp-controller="Messages"
                                  asp-action="DisableMessageTemplate"
                                  asp-route-id="@autoReplyTemplate.TemplateID"
                                  method="post"
                                  class="d-inline">
                                @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                                <button type="submit" class="btn btn-outline-warning btn-sm">
                                    禁用
                                </button>
                            </form>
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            <form asp-controller="Messages"
                                  asp-action="EnableMessageTemplate"
                                  asp-route-id="@autoReplyTemplate.TemplateID"
                                  method="post"
                                  class="d-inline">
                                @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                                <button type="submit" class="btn btn-outline-success btn-sm">
                                    啟用
                                </button>
                            </form>
                        }
                    </div>
                </div>
            }
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- ============================= -->
    <!-- 快速回覆模板 -->
    <!-- ============================= -->
    <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
        <div class="card-header bg-white d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">
            <div>
                <div class="fw-bold">快速回覆模板</div>
                <div class="small text-muted">可新增、修改、啟用 / 禁用</div>
            </div>

            <a asp-controller="Messages"
               asp-action="CreateMessageTemplate"
               class="btn btn-primary btn-sm">
                <i class="bi bi-plus-circle me-1"></i> 新增
            </a>
        </div>

        <div class="card-body">
            @if (!quickReplyTemplates.Any())
            {
                <div class="text-center text-muted py-4">
                    目前還沒有快速回覆模板
                </div>
            }
            else
            {
                <div class="row g-3">
                    @foreach (var item in quickReplyTemplates)
                    {
                        <div class="col-md-6">
                            <div class="border rounded p-3 h-100">
                                <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-start mb-2">
                                    <div class="fw-semibold">@item.Title</div>

                                    @if (item.IsActive)
                                    {
                                        <span class="badge bg-success">啟用</span>
                                    }
                                    else
                                    {
                                        <span class="badge bg-secondary">禁用</span>
                                    }
                                </div>

                                <div class="text-muted small mb-2">
                                    類型：@item.TriggerTypeName
                                </div>

                                <div class="mb-3" style="white-space: pre-wrap;">@item.Content</div>

                                <div class="d-flex gap-2">
                                    <a asp-controller="Messages"
                                       asp-action="EditMessageTemplate"
                                       asp-route-id="@item.TemplateID"
                                       class="btn btn-outline-primary btn-sm">
                                        編輯
                                    </a>

                                    @if (item.IsActive)
                                    {
                                        <form asp-controller="Messages"
                                              asp-action="DisableMessageTemplate"
                                              asp-route-id="@item.TemplateID"
                                              method="post"
                                              class="d-inline">

@model CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.Notification.VMNotificationIndex
@using CraftDailyCorner.Models.enums
@using CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.Notification

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "我的通知";
}

<div class="container py-4">

    @if (TempData["Success"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-success">@TempData["Success"]</div>
    }

    <div class="d-flex flex-column flex-lg-row justify-content-between align-items-lg-center gap-3 mb-4">
        <div>
            <h2 class="fw-bold mb-1">我的通知</h2>
            <div class="text-muted">
                未讀通知：@Model.UnreadCount
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="d-flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <a asp-controller="Notification"
               asp-action="Index"
               asp-route-unreadOnly="false"
               asp-route-filterType="@Model.FilterType"
               class="btn @(Model.UnreadOnly ? "btn-outline-dark" : "btn-dark")">
                全部
            </a>

            <a asp-controller="Notification"
               asp-action="Index"
               asp-route-unreadOnly="true"
               asp-route-filterType="@Model.FilterType"
               class="btn @(Model.UnreadOnly ? "btn-warning" : "btn-outline-warning")">
                只看未讀
            </a>

            <form asp-controller="Notification"
                  asp-action="MarkAllAsRead"
                  method="post"
                  class="d-inline">
                @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                <button type="submit"
                        class="btn btn-outline-primary"
                        @(Model.UnreadCount == 0 ? "disabled" : null)>
                    全部標記為已讀
                </button>
            </form>

            <a asp-controller="Notification"
               asp-action="Preferences"
               class="btn btn-outline-secondary">
                通知設定
            </a>
        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="d-flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-3">
        <a asp-controller="Notification"
           asp-action="Index"
           asp-route-unreadOnly="@Model.UnreadOnly"
           asp-route-filterType="All"
           class="btn btn-sm @(Model.FilterType == NotificationFilterType.All ? "btn-dark" : "btn-outline-dark")">
            全部類型
        </a>

        <a asp-controller="Notification"
           asp-action="Index"
           asp-route-unreadOnly="@Model.UnreadOnly"
           asp-route-filterType="Announcement"
           class="btn btn-sm @(Model.FilterType == NotificationFilterType.Announcement ? "btn-primary" : "btn-outline-primary")">
            公告
        </a>

        <a asp-controller="Notification"
           asp-action="Index"
           asp-route-unreadOnly="@Model.UnreadOnly"
           asp-route-filterType="Order"
           class="btn btn-sm @(Model.FilterType == NotificationFilterType.Order ? "btn-success" : "btn-outline-success")">
            訂單
        </a>

        <a asp-controller="Notification"
           asp-action="Index"
           asp-route-unreadOnly="@Model.UnreadOnly"
           asp-route-filterType="Product"
           class="btn btn-sm @(Model.FilterType == NotificationFilterType.Product ? "btn-warning" : "btn-outline-warning")">
            商品
        </a>

        <a asp-controller="Notification"
           asp-action="Index"
           asp-route-unreadOnly="@Model.UnreadOnly"
           asp-route-filterType="Creator"
           class="btn btn-sm @(Model.FilterType == NotificationFilterType.Creator ? "btn-info" : "btn-outline-info")">
            創作者互動
        </a>
    </div>

    <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
        <div class="card-body p-0">

            @if (!Model.Items.Any())
            {
                <div class="p-5 text-center text-muted">
                    <i class="bi bi-bell fs-2 d-block mb-2"></i>
                    目前沒有符合條件的通知
                </div>
            }
            else
            {
                <div class="list-group list-group-flush">
                    @foreach (var item in Model.Items)
                    {
                        var targetUrl = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(item.LinkUrl)
                        ? Url.Action("Index", "Notification")
                        : item.LinkUrl;

                        var typeClass = item.NotificationType switch
                        {
                            CraftDailyCorner.Models.enums.NotificationType.Announcement => "notification-blue",
                            CraftDailyCorner.Models.enums.NotificationType.OrderCompleted => "notification-blue",

                            CraftDailyCorner.Models.enums.NotificationType.OrderCreated => "notification-green",
                            CraftDailyCorner.Models.enums.NotificationType.OrderPaid => "notification-green",
                            CraftDailyCorner.Models.enums.NotificationType.OrderShipped => "notification-green",
                            CraftDailyCorner.Models.enums.NotificationType.OrderDelivered => "notification-green",

                            CraftDailyCorner.Models.enums.NotificationType.ProductOutOfStock => "notification-red",

                            CraftDailyCorner.Models.enums.NotificationType.ProductLowStock => "notification-yellow",

                            CraftDailyCorner.Models.enums.NotificationType.FavoriteProductPublished => "notification-info",
                            CraftDailyCorner.Models.enums.NotificationType.FavoriteProductRestocked => "notification-info",
                            CraftDailyCorner.Models.enums.NotificationType.CreatorNewPost => "notification-info",
                            CraftDailyCorner.Models.enums.NotificationType.CreatorNewProduct => "notification-info",
                            CraftDailyCorner.Models.enums.NotificationType.CreatorNewPortfolio => "notification-info",
                            CraftDailyCorner.Models.enums.NotificationType.PostComment => "notification-info",

                            _ => "notification-default"
                        };

                        <a href="@targetUrl"
                           class="list-group-item list-group-item-action notification-center-link notification-center-item @(item.IsRead ? "" : "notification-unread")"
                           data-event-id="@item.EventID"
                           data-is-read="@item.IsRead.ToString().ToLower()">

                            <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-start gap-3 mb-2">
                                <div class="d-flex align-items-center gap-2 flex-grow-1 min-w-0">
                                    <i class="bi bi-broadcast text-muted flex-shrink-0"></i>
                                    <span class="notification-title @typeClass">
                                        @item.Title
                                    </span>
                                </div>

                                <small class="text-muted flex-shrink-0">
                                    @item.CreatedAt.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm")
                                </small>
                            </div>

                            <div class="text-muted small notification-content">
                                @item.Content
                            </div>

                            @if (!item.IsRead)
                            {
                                <div class="mt-2">
                                    <span class="badge bg-danger">未讀</span>
                                </div>
                            }
                        </a>
                    }
                </div>
            }
        </div>
    </div>

    @if (Model.TotalPages > 1)
    {
        <div class="mt-4 d-flex flex-column flex-md-row justify-content-between align-items-md-center gap-3">
            <div class="text-muted small">
                第 @Model.Page / @Model.TotalPages 頁，共 @Model.TotalCount 筆
            </div>

            <nav>
                <ul class="pagination mb-0">
                    <li class="page-item @(Model.Page <= 1 ? "disabled" : "")">
                        <a class="page-link"
                           asp-controller="Notification"
                           asp-action="Index"
                           asp-route-page="@(Model.Page - 1)"
                           asp-route-unreadOnly="@Model.UnreadOnly"
                           asp-route-filterType="@Model.FilterType">
                            上一頁
                        </a>
                    </li>

                    @for (int p = 1; p <= Model.TotalPages; p++)
                    {
                        <li class="page-item @(p == Model.Page ? "active" : "")">
                            <a class="page-link"
                               asp-controller="Notification"
                               asp-action="Index"
                               asp-route-page="@p"
                               asp-route-unreadOnly="@Model.UnreadOnly"
                               asp-route-filterType="@Model.FilterType">
                                @p
                            </a>
                        </li>
                    }

                    <li class="page-item @(Model.Page >= Model.TotalPages ? "disabled" : "")">
                        <a class="page-link"
                           asp-controller="Notification"
                           asp-action="Index"
                           asp-route-page="@(Model.Page + 1)"
                           asp-route-unreadOnly="@Model.UnreadOnly"
                           asp-route-filterType="@Model.FilterType">
                            下一頁
                        </a>
                    </li>
                </ul>
            </nav>
        </div>
    }
</div>

<script>
    (function () {
        const links = document.querySelectorAll(".notification-center-link");

        links.forEach(link => {
            link.addEventListener("click", function () {
                const eventId = this.dataset.eventId;
                const isRead = this.dataset.isRead === "true";

                if (!eventId || isRead) return;

                navigator.sendBeacon(`/Notification/MarkAsRead/${eventId}`);
            });
        });
    })();
</script>

                        </tr>
                    </tfoot>

        </div>

@section Scripts {

    <script>
            window.addEventListener('cartUpdated', function(e) {
            const totalQty = e.detail.totalQuantity;
            if (totalQty === 0) {
                window.location.href = '/Products/Index';
            } else {
                // 在結帳頁面直接執行重新整理
                window.location.reload();
            }
        });
    </script>

                        <a asp-controller="Orders"
                           asp-action="Index"
                           class="btn btn-outline-secondary">
                            查看我的訂單
                        </a>
                    </div>

                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

FILE: CraftDailyCorner\Views\Orders\Detail.cshtml

@using CraftDailyCorner.Services.Interface
@using CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.Member
@model VMMyOrderDetail
@inject ISiteSettingService SiteSetting

@{
    var email = await SiteSetting.GetStringAsync("PlatformServiceEmail");
}
@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "訂單明細";

    int step = Model.StatusID switch
    {
        1 => 1, // 待付款
        2 => 2, // 已付款
        3 => 3, // 準備中
        4 => (Model.ShipmentStatusID == 3 ? 5 : 4), // 已出貨  //判斷商品是否已送達
        5 => 5, // 完成
        6 => 6, // 取消
        _ => 0
    };

    // 判斷狀態
    bool isPendingPayment = Model.StatusID == 1;
    bool isShipped = Model.StatusID == 4;
    bool isCompleted = Model.StatusID == 5;
    bool isCancelled = Model.StatusID == 6;

    // 根據狀態設定 Banner 樣式
    string bannerClass = "alert-success";
    string iconClass = "bi-check-circle";
    string subMessage = "此訂單已完成付款，感謝您的購買";


    if (isPendingPayment)
    {
        bannerClass = "alert-warning";
        iconClass = "bi-exclamation-circle";
        subMessage = "此訂單尚未完成付款，請於下方進行付款";
    }
    else if (isShipped)
    {
        bannerClass = "alert-info";
        iconClass = "bi-truck";

        if (Model.ShipmentStatusID == 3)
        {
            subMessage = "您的訂單已送達，請於規定時間內完成取貨";
        }
        else
        {
            subMessage = "您的訂單已出貨，請耐心等待配送";
        }
    }
    else if (isCompleted)
    {
        bannerClass = "alert-success";
        iconClass = "bi-check-circle-fill";
        subMessage = "此訂單已完成，感謝您的購買！";
    }else if (isCancelled)
    {
        bannerClass = "alert-secondary";
        iconClass = "bi-x-circle";
        subMessage = "此訂單已取消，相關商品庫存已釋出，歡迎重新選購。";
    }

    // 訂單狀態 badge 顏色
    string statusBadgeClass = Model.StatusText switch
    {
        "待付款" => "bg-danger",   // 紅色
        "已付款" => "bg-success",   // 綠色
        "準備中" => "bg-success",   
        "已出貨" => "bg-warning",   // 黃色
        "完成" => "bg-primary",   // 藍色
        _ => "bg-secondary"
    };
}

<div class="container my-5">

    <!-- ===== 標題列 ===== -->
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
        <h3 class="fw-bold">訂單明細</h3>

        <a asp-action="Index"
           class="btn btn-outline-secondary btn-sm fs-5">
            返回我的訂單
        </a>
    </div>

    <!-- ===== 訂單狀態 Banner ===== -->
    <div class="alert @bannerClass d-flex align-items-center mb-4 shadow-sm">
        <i class="bi @iconClass fs-4 me-3"></i>
        <div>
            <div class="fw-semibold fs-5">
                @Model.StatusText
            </div>
            <small class="text-muted fs-6">
                @subMessage
            </small>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- ===== 訂單狀態進度條 ===== -->
    @if (Model.StatusID != 6)
    {
        <div class="card mb-4 shadow-sm">
            <div class="card-body">
                <div class="order-progress">
                    <div class="step @(step >= 1 ? "active" : "")">
                        <div class="circle">1</div>
                        <div class="label">已下單<br /><small>建立訂單</small></div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="line @(step >= 2 ? "active" : "")"></div>

                    <div class="step @(step >= 2 ? "active" : "")">
                        <div class="circle">2</div>
                        <div class="label">已付款<br /><small>付款完成</small></div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="line @(step >= 3 ? "active" : "")"></div>

                    <div class="step @(step >= 3 ? "active" : "")">
                        <div class="circle">3</div>
                        <div class="label">準備中<br /><small>訂單準備中</small></div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="line @(step >= 4 ? "active" : "")"></div>

                    <div class="step @(step >= 4 ? "active" : "")">
                        <div class="circle">4</div>
                        <div class="label">配送中<br /><small>商品寄出</small></div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="line @(step >= 5 ? "active" : "")"></div>

                    <div class="step @(step >= 5 ? "active" : "")">
                        <div class="circle">5</div>
                        <div class="label">已完成<br /><small>完成收貨</small></div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    }
    <!-- ===== 訂單基本資訊 ===== -->
    @{
        // 只要進入配送中，就切成 4/4/4 並顯示右側動作區
        bool showAction = (Model.StatusID == 4);

        string colInfo = showAction ? "col-md-4" : "col-md-6";
    }

    <div class="card mb-4 shadow-sm">
        <div class="card-body">
            <div class="row gy-3 align-items-stretch">

                <!-- 訂單資訊 -->
                <div class="@colInfo">
                    <div class="fw-semibold">訂單編號</div>
                    <div class="text-muted">@Model.OrderID</div>

                    <div class="fw-semibold mt-3">下單時間</div>
                    <div class="text-muted">
                        @Model.CreatedAt.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm")
                    </div>

                    <div class="fw-semibold mt-3">訂單狀態</div>
                    <span class="badge @statusBadgeClass">
                        @Model.StatusText
                    </span>

                    @if (Model.DeliveredAt != null)
                    {
                        <div class="text-muted small mt-2">
                            送達時間：@Model.DeliveredAt?.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm")
                        </div>
                    }
                </div>

                <!-- 收件資訊 -->
                <div class="@colInfo">
                    <div class="fw-semibold">收件人</div>
                    <div class="text-muted">@Model.ReceiverName</div>

                    <div class="fw-semibold mt-3">聯絡電話</div>
                    <div class="text-muted">@Model.ReceiverPhone</div>

                    <div class="fw-semibold mt-3">配送地址</div>
                    <div class="text-muted">@Model.ShippingAddress</div>
                </div>

                <!-- 動作區：只有特定條件才出現 -->
                @if (showAction)
                {
                    <div class="col-md-4 d-flex justify-content-md-end">
                        <div class="d-flex flex-column gap-2 w-100">

                            @* 配送中才顯示「取貨」 *@
                            @if (Model.StatusID == 4 && Model.ShipmentStatusID == 3)
                            {
                                <form asp-action="ConfirmPickup" method="post" class="d-flex justify-content-end">
                                    @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                                    <input type="hidden" name="orderId" value="@Model.OrderID" />

                                    <button type="submit" class="order-card-btn order-card-blue">
                                        <div class="card-icon">
                                            <i class="bi bi-box-seam"></i>
                                        </div>
                                        <div class="card-text">
                                            取貨（模擬）
                                        </div>
                                    </button>
                                </form>
                            }

                        </div>
                    </div>
                }

            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- ===== 訂單總金額 ===== -->
    <div class="card mb-4 border-0 shadow-sm">
        <div class="card-body text-center">
            <div class="text-muted mb-1">訂單總金額</div>
            <div class="fs-2 fw-bold text-danger">
                NT$ @Model.TotalAmount.ToString("N0")
            </div>
            <!-- ===== 查看付款紀錄 ===== -->
            <button class="btn btn-outline-primary mt-2"
                    data-bs-toggle="collapse"
                    data-bs-target="#orderPaymentCollapse">
                查看此訂單的付款紀錄
            </button>
        </div>
    </div>
    <div class="collapse mt-3" id="orderPaymentCollapse">
        @await Html.PartialAsync("_OrderPaymentPartial", Model.OrderPayments)
    </div>
    <!-- ===== 商品明細 ===== -->
    <div class="card mb-4 shadow-sm">
        <div class="card-body p-0">
            <table class="table mb-0 align-middle table-hover">
                <thead class="table-light">
                    <tr>
                        <th>商品</th>
                        <th class="text-end">單價</th>
                        <th class="text-end">數量</th>
                        <th class="text-end">小計</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                    @foreach (var item in Model.Items)
                    {
                        <tr>
                            <td>
                                <div class="fw-semibold">@item.ProductName</div>
                                <small class="text-muted">創作者：@item.BrandName</small>
                            </td>
                            <td class="text-end">$@item.Price</td>
                            <td class="text-end">@item.Quantity</td>
                            <td class="text-end fw-semibold">$@item.SubTotal</td>
                        </tr>
                    }
                </tbody>
            </table>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- ===== 付款主行動 ===== -->
    <div class="text-center my-5">
        @if (Model.StatusID == 1) @* 待付款 *@
        {
            <a asp-controller="Payment"
               asp-action="Create"
               asp-route-orderId="@Model.OrderID"
               class="btn btn-primary btn-lg px-5 py-3 shadow me-3">
                <i class="bi bi-credit-card me-2"></i>
                前往付款
            </a>
            <div class="text-muted small mt-2">
                支援多種付款方式，安全快速
            </div>

            <form asp-action="Cancel" method="post" class="d-inline"
                  onsubmit="return confirm('確定要取消這筆訂單嗎？')">
                @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                <input type="hidden" name="orderId" value="@Model.OrderID" />
                <button type="submit" class="btn btn-outline-danger btn-lg px-4 py-3">
                    取消訂單
                </button>
            </form>
        }
        else if (Model.StatusID == 6)
        {
            <div class="alert alert-secondary text-center shadow-sm py-4">
                <i class="bi bi-x-circle-fill fs-3 d-block mb-2"></i>
                <span class="fs-5">此訂單已取消，相關商品庫存已釋出，歡迎重新選購。</span>
            </div>
        }
        else
        {
            @if (Model.StatusID != 5)
            {
                <div class="alert alert-success text-center shadow-sm">
                    <i class="bi bi-check-circle-fill fs-3 me-2"></i>
                    此訂單已付款，系統正持續處理中
                </div>                
            }
        }
    </div>

    <!-- ===== 客服資訊 ===== -->
    <!-- ===== 客服資訊 ===== -->
    <div class="alert alert-light mt-4">
        <div class="fw-semibold mb-1">訂單有問題？</div>
        <small class="text-muted">
            若您對訂單內容或配送狀態有任何疑問，請聯絡客服：
            @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(email))
            {
                <a href="mailto:@email">@email</a>
            }
            else
            {
                <span>目前尚未設定客服信箱</span>
            }
        </small>
    </div>

</div>

        <a asp-controller="Member"
           asp-action="Index"
           class="btn btn-outline-secondary btn-sm fs-5">
            返回會員中心
        </a>
    </div>
    
    @if (TempData["Success"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-success alert-dismissible fade show" role="alert">
            <i class="bi bi-check-circle me-2"></i> @TempData["Success"]
            <button type="button" class="btn-close" data-bs-dismiss="alert" aria-label="Close"></button>
        </div>
    }
    @if (TempData["Error"] != null)
    {
        <div class="alert alert-danger alert-dismissible fade show" role="alert">
            <i class="bi bi-exclamation-triangle me-2"></i> @TempData["Error"]
            <button type="button" class="btn-close" data-bs-dismiss="alert" aria-label="Close"></button>
        </div>
    }

    @if (!Model.Any())
    {
        @* <div class="alert alert-info">
            目前尚無訂單紀錄
        </div> *@
        <div class="text-center py-5 border rounded bg-light">
            <i class="bi bi-receipt text-muted" style="font-size: 3rem;"></i>
            <p class="mt-3 text-muted">目前尚無訂單紀錄</p>
            <a asp-controller="Products" asp-action="Index" class="btn btn-primary">去逛逛商品</a>
        </div>
    }
    else
    {
        <div class="table-responsive">
            <table class="table align-middle">
                <thead class="table-light">
                    <tr>
                        <th>訂單編號</th>
                        <th>下單時間</th>
                        <th class="text-end">總金額</th>
                        <th>狀態</th>
                        <th class="text-end">操作</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                    @foreach (var order in Model)
                    {
                        <tr>
                            <td>
                                <span class="fw-semibold">
                                    @order.OrderID
                                </span>
                            </td>

                            <td>
                                @order.CreatedAt.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm")
                            </td>

                            <td class="text-end">
                                <span class="fw-semibold">
                                    $@order.TotalAmount
                                </span>
                            </td>

                            <td>
                                @{
                                    string statusBadgeClass = order.StatusText switch
                                    {
                                        "待付款" => "bg-danger",   // 紅色 (需警示)
                                        "已付款" => "bg-success",  // 綠色
                                        "準備中" => "bg-success",  // 綠色
                                        "已出貨" => "bg-warning text-dark", // 黃色
                                        "完成" => "bg-primary",  // 藍色
                                        "取消" => "bg-secondary", // 灰色
                                        _ => "bg-secondary"
                                    };
                                }
                                <span class="badge @statusBadgeClass" ">
                                    @if (order.StatusText == "取消")
                                    {
                                        @order.StatusText <span>-</span> @order.UpdatedAt.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm")
                                    }
                                    else
                                    {
                                        @order.StatusText
                                    }
                                    
                                </span>
                            </td>

                            <td class="text-end">
                                <a asp-action="Detail"
                                   asp-route-orderId="@order.OrderID"
                                   class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary">
                                    查看明細
                                </a>
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                    }
                </tbody>
            </table>
        </div>
    }

    
</div>

@using CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.Payment
@model VMPaymentCreate

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "付款";
}

<div class="container mt-4">
    <h2 class="mb-4">訂單付款</h2>

    <!-- 訂單資訊 -->
    <div class="card mb-4">
        <div class="card-body">
            <p><strong>訂單編號：</strong>@Model.OrderID</p>
            <p><strong>訂單成立時間：</strong>@Model.OrderCreatedAt.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm")</p>
            <p class="fs-5">
                <strong>付款金額：</strong>
                <span class="text-danger fw-bold">
                    NT$ @Model.OrderAmount.ToString("N0")
                </span>
            </p>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- 付款表單 -->
    <form asp-action="Create" method="post">
        @Html.AntiForgeryToken()

        <input type="hidden" name="OrderID" value="@Model.OrderID" />

        <!-- 付款方式 -->
        <div class="card mb-4 border-primary shadow-sm">
            <div class="card-body">
                <div class="d-flex align-items-center mb-3">
                    <i class="bi bi-credit-card fs-3 text-primary me-2"></i>
                    <label class="form-label fs-4 fw-semibold mb-0">
                        選擇付款方式
                    </label>
                </div>

                <select name="MethodID" class="form-select form-select-lg" required>
                    <option value="">請選擇付款方式</option>
                    @foreach (var method in Model.PaymentMethods)
                    {
                        <option value="@method.MethodID">@method.MethodName</option>
                    }
                </select>

                <small class="text-muted mt-2 d-block">
                    請選擇您要使用的付款方式完成交易
                </small>
            </div>
        </div>
        <!-- Mock Payment -->
        <div class="mb-4 mt-5">
            <label class="form-label">模擬付款結果</label>
            <div class="form-check">
                <input class="form-check-input" type="radio" name="SimulateSuccess" value="true" checked />
                <label class="form-check-label">
                    模擬付款成功
                </label>
            </div>
            <div class="form-check">
                <input class="form-check-input" type="radio" name="SimulateSuccess" value="false" />
                <label class="form-check-label">
                    模擬付款失敗
                </label>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 錯誤訊息 -->
        @if (TempData["PaymentError"] != null)
        {
            <div class="alert alert-danger">
                @TempData["PaymentError"]
            </div>
        }

        <!-- 操作按鈕 -->
        <div class="d-flex gap-2">
            <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary">
                確認付款
            </button>
            <a asp-controller="Orders"
               asp-action="Detail"
               asp-route-orderId="@Model.OrderID"
               class="btn btn-outline-secondary">
                返回訂單
            </a>
        </div>
    </form>
</div>

@using CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.Payment
@model List<VMPaymentIndexItem>

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "付款紀錄";
}

<div class="container my-5">

    <!-- 標題 -->
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
        <h3 class="fw-bold">
            <i class="bi bi-clock-history me-2 text-secondary"> </i>
            付款紀錄
            </h3>

        <a asp-controller="Member"
           asp-action="Index"
           class="btn btn-outline-secondary btn-sm fs-5">
            返回會員中心
        </a>
    </div>

    @if (!Model.Any())
    {
        <div class="alert alert-info">
            目前尚無付款紀錄
        </div>

            <div class="card-body p-0">

                <table class="table mb-0 align-middle table-hover">
                    <thead class="table-light">
                        <tr>
                            <th>訂單編號</th>
                            <th>付款方式</th>
                            <th class="text-end">金額</th>
                            <th>狀態</th>
                            <th>建立時間</th>
                            <th>付款時間</th>
                            <th class="text-end">操作</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                        @foreach (var p in Model)
                        {
                            <tr>
                                <td class="fw-semibold">
                                    @p.OrderID
                                </td>

                                <td>
                                    @p.MethodName
                                </td>

                                <td class="text-end">
                                    NT$ @p.Amount.ToString("N0")
                                </td>

                                <td>
                                    @if (p.PaidAt.HasValue)
                                    {
                                        <span class="badge bg-success">
                                            @p.StatusName
                                        </span>
                                    }
                                    else
                                    {
                                        <span class="badge bg-secondary">
                                            @p.StatusName
                                        </span>
                                    }
                            </td>

                            <td>
                                @p.CreatedAt.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm")
                            </td>

                            <td>
                                @(p.PaidAt.HasValue
                                                            ? p.PaidAt.Value.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm")
                                                            : "-")
                                </td>

                                <td class="text-end">
                                    <a asp-controller="Orders"
                                       asp-action="Detail"
                                       asp-route-orderId="@p.OrderID"
                                       class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary">
                                        查看訂單
                                    </a>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        }
                    </tbody>
                </table>

            </div>
        </div>
    }

FILE: CraftDailyCorner\Views\Payment\Result.cshtml
==================================================

@using CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.Payment
@model VMPaymentResult

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "付款結果";
    bool isSuccess = Model.PaidAt.HasValue;
}

<div class="container mt-5 text-center">

    @if (isSuccess)
    {
        <div class="mb-4">
            <i class="bi bi-check-circle-fill text-success fs-1"></i>
        </div>
        <h2 class="text-success mb-3">付款成功</h2>
    }
    else
    {
        <div class="mb-4">
            <i class="bi bi-x-circle-fill text-danger fs-1"></i>
        </div>
        <h2 class="text-danger mb-3">付款失敗</h2>
    }

    <p class="mb-2">
        <strong>訂單編號：</strong>@Model.OrderID
    </p>

    <p class="mb-2">
        <strong>付款金額：</strong>
        NT$ @Model.Amount.ToString("N0")
    </p>

    @if (Model.PaidAt.HasValue)
    {
        <p class="mb-4">
            <strong>付款時間：</strong>
            @Model.PaidAt.Value.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm")
        </p>
    }

    <p class="mb-4">@Model.Message</p>

    <div class="d-flex justify-content-center gap-2">
        <a asp-controller="Orders"
           asp-action="Detail"
           asp-route-orderId="@Model.OrderID"
           class="btn btn-outline-primary">
            查看訂單
        </a>

        @if (!isSuccess)
        {
            <a asp-action="Create"
               asp-route-orderId="@Model.OrderID"
               class="btn btn-primary">
                重新付款
            </a>
        }
    </div>
</div>

FILE: CraftDailyCorner\Views\Portfolio\Create.cshtml
==================================================

@model CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.CreatorPortfolio.VMCreatorPortfolioCreate
@using CraftDailyCorner.Models.enums;
@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "新增作品集";
}

<div class="container py-5" style="max-width:900px;">

    <!-- 標題 -->
    <div class="mb-4">
        <h2 class="fw-bold">新增作品集</h2>
        <p class="text-muted mb-0">建立新的作品集</p>
    </div>

    <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
        <div class="card-body p-4">

            <form asp-action="Create"
                  method="post"
                  enctype="multipart/form-data"
                  class="needs-validation"
                  novalidate>

                @Html.AntiForgeryToken()

                <!-- Validation Summary -->
                <div asp-validation-summary="ModelOnly"
                     class="text-danger mb-3"></div>

                <!-- 標題 -->
                <div class="mb-3">
                    <label asp-for="Title"
                           class="form-label fw-semibold"></label>
                    <input asp-for="Title"
                           class="form-control" />
                    <span asp-validation-for="Title"
                          class="text-danger small"></span>
                </div>

                <!-- 描述 -->
                <div class="mb-3">
                    <label asp-for="Description"
                           class="form-label fw-semibold"></label>
                    <textarea asp-for="Description"
                              class="form-control"
                              rows="4"></textarea>
                    <span asp-validation-for="Description"
                          class="text-danger small"></span>
                </div>

                <!-- 可見性 -->
                <div class="mb-3">
                    <label asp-for="Visibility"
                           class="form-label fw-semibold"></label>
                    <select asp-for="Visibility"
                            asp-items="Html.GetEnumSelectList<CreatorPostVisibility>()"
                            class="form-select">
                    </select>
                </div>

                <!-- 圖片上傳 -->
                <div class="mb-4">
                    <label asp-for="Files"
                           class="form-label fw-semibold"></label>

                    <input type="file"
                           asp-for="Files"
                           multiple
                           id="portfolio-image-input"
                           class="form-control" />

                    <div class="form-text">
                        可選擇多張圖片（最多 25 張）
                    </div>

                    <span asp-validation-for="Files"
                          class="text-danger small"></span>
                    <div>作品預覽：</div>
                    <!-- 新增這個預覽區 -->
                    <div id="portfolio-preview-container"
                         class="row mt-3"></div>
                </div>

                <!-- 按鈕 -->
                <div class="d-flex gap-2">
                    <button type="submit"
                            class="btn btn-dark">
                        <i class="bi bi-plus-lg me-1"></i>
                        建立作品集
                    </button>

                    <a asp-action="List"
                       class="btn btn-outline-secondary">
                        返回列表
                    </a>
                </div>

            </form>

        </div>
    </div>

</div>

@section Scripts {
    <partial name="_ValidationScriptsPartial" />

    <script>
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {

            const form = document.querySelector("form");
            const fileInput = document.getElementById("portfolio-image-input");
            const previewContainer = document.getElementById("portfolio-preview-container");
            const maxFiles = 25;

            // ===============================
            // 多圖預覽
            // ===============================
            fileInput.addEventListener("change", function () {

                previewContainer.innerHTML = "";

                const fileCount = this.files.length;

                if (fileCount > maxFiles) {
                    alert(`圖片最多只能上傳 ${maxFiles} 張`);
                    this.value = "";
                    return;
                }

                Array.from(this.files).forEach(file => {

                    const reader = new FileReader();

                    reader.onload = function (e) {

                        const col = document.createElement("div");
                        col.className = "col-md-3 mb-3";

                        col.innerHTML = `
                            <img src="${e.target.result}"
                                 class="img-fluid rounded shadow-sm"
                                 style="height:160px;object-fit:cover;" />
                        `;

                        previewContainer.appendChild(col);
                    };

                    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                });
            });

            // ===============================
            // 送出前驗證
            // ===============================
            form.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {

                let hasError = false;

                const fileError =
                    document.querySelector("[data-valmsg-for='Files']");

                // 清空舊錯誤
                fileError.textContent = "";
                fileError.classList.remove("field-validation-error");
                fileError.classList.add("field-validation-valid");

                const fileCount = fileInput.files.length;

                // 至少 1 張
                if (fileCount === 0) {
                    fileError.textContent = "請至少上傳 1 張圖片";
                    fileError.classList.remove("field-validation-valid");
                    fileError.classList.add("field-validation-error");
                    hasError = true;
                }

                // 不得超過 25 張
                if (fileCount > maxFiles) {
                    fileError.textContent = `圖片最多只能上傳 ${maxFiles} 張`;
                    fileError.classList.remove("field-validation-valid");
                    fileError.classList.add("field-validation-error");
                    hasError = true;
                }

                if (hasError) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                }
            });

        });
    </script>
}

}

{
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="~/css/PortfolioDetail.css" />
}

    <!-- 標題區 -->
    <div class="mb-5">

                </h2>

                             model="Model.ReactionButton" />
                </div>

                 @if (User.Identity?.IsAuthenticated == true)

                                 {
                                     ReportType = ReportTargetType.Portfolio,
                                     TargetID = Model.PortfolioID,

                                 }" />
                    }
                }
            </div>

        </div>
        <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">
            <div class="text-muted text-content">
                @Model.Description              
            </div>
            <div class="small text-secondary">
                <a asp-controller="Creator" asp-action="Profile" asp-route-id="@Model.CreatorID"
                   class="text-decoration-none text-success">
                    @Model.CreatorName
                </a>
                · @Model.CreatedAt.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd")
            </div>
        </div>
        
    </div>

    <!-- 畫廊區 -->
    <div id="Portfoliocarousel" 
         class="carousel slide carousel-fade rounded-4 overflow-hidden shadow-sm" 
         data-bs-ride="carousel">
        <div class="carousel-inner">
            @foreach (var item in Model.Items)
            {
                var a = item == Model.Items.First() ? "active" : "";

                <div class="carousel-item @a">
                    <img src="@($"/Photos/06Portfolio/Large/{item.ImageUrl}.png")"
                         class="d-block w-100" />
                </div>
            }
            

        </div>
        <button class="carousel-control-prev" type="button" data-bs-target="#Portfoliocarousel" data-bs-slide="prev">
            <span class="carousel-control-prev-icon" aria-hidden="true"></span>
            <span class="visually-hidden">Previous</span>
        </button>
        <button class="carousel-control-next" type="button" data-bs-target="#Portfoliocarousel" data-bs-slide="next">
            <span class="carousel-control-next-icon" aria-hidden="true"></span>
            <span class="visually-hidden">Next</span>
        </button>
    </div>
    <!-- 喜歡提示 + Reaction 按鈕 -->
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-center align-items-center gap-3 mt-4">

        <div class="text-muted">
            喜歡這個作品集嗎？
        </div>

        <div class="big-effect">
            <partial name="_ReactionButtonPartial"
                     model="Model.ReactionButton" />
        </div>

    </div>

</div>

                       class="btn btn-outline-secondary">
                        返回列表
                    </a>
                </div>

        </div>

                <!-- 由 AJAX 載入 -->
            </div>

        </div>

@section Scripts {

    <partial name="_ValidationScriptsPartial" />

    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/sortablejs@1.15.2/Sortable.min.js"></script>
    <script src="~/js/imageManagement.js"></script>

    <script>
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", async function () {

            const portfolioId = "@Model.PortfolioID";

            const response = await fetch(
                `/ImageManagement/PortfolioPartial/${portfolioId}`
            );

            const html = await response.text();

            document.getElementById("imageManagementContainer")
                    .innerHTML = html;

            if (typeof initImageManagement === "function") {
                initImageManagement();
            }
        });
    </script>
}

@model CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.CreatorPortfolio.Front.VMPortfolioIndex

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "作品集";
}

<div class="container py-5" style="max-width:1100px;">

    <!-- 標題 -->
    <div class="text-center mb-2">
        <h2 class="fw-bold">作品集</h2>
        <p class="text-muted">探索最新作品分類</p>
    </div>

    <!-- 搜尋 -->
    <form method="get" class="row justify-content-center mb-3">
        <div class="col-md-6 d-flex gap-2">
            <input type="text"
                   name="keyword"
                   value="@Model.Query.Keyword"
                   class="form-control"
                   placeholder="搜尋標題或內容..." />

            <button type="submit"
                    class="btn btn-dark text-nowrap px-4">
                <i class="bi bi-search me-1"></i>搜尋
            </button>
        </div>
    </form>

    @if (!Model.Portfolios.Any())
    {
        <div class="text-center text-muted py-5">
            尚無符合條件的作品集
        </div>
    }
    else
    {
        <div class="row g-4">
            @foreach (var portfolio in Model.Portfolios)
            {
                <div class="col-md-6 col-lg-4">
                    <div class="card shadow-sm border-0 h-100 p-card">

                        <!-- ✅整張卡都是連結 -->
                        <a asp-action="Detail"
                           asp-route-id="@portfolio.PortfolioID"
                           class="text-decoration-none text-dark">

                            <!-- 封面圖片 -->
                            <img src="@portfolio.CoverImagePath"
                                 class="card-img-top"
                                 style="height:240px; object-fit:cover;" />

                            <div class="card-body d-flex flex-column">

                                <!-- 第1行：標題(左) + Reaction(右) -->
                                <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-2">

                                    <div class="fw-semibold h5 mb-0 text-truncate flex-grow-1 me-2">
                                        <i class="bi bi-filter-right"></i>
                                        @portfolio.Title
                                        <i class="bi bi-filter-left"></i>
                                    </div>

                                    <div class="ms-2 flex-shrink-0">
                                        <partial name="_ReactionSummaryPartial"
                                                 model="portfolio.ReactionSummary" />
                                    </div>
                                </div>

                                <!-- 第2行：內文預覽(左) -->
                                <div class="text-muted small p-preview mb-2">
                                    @portfolio.Preview
                                </div>

                                <!-- 第3行：作者 + 日期（靠右） -->
                                <div class="text-muted small text-end mt-auto">
                                    <a asp-controller="Creator" asp-action="Profile" asp-route-id="@portfolio.CreatorID"
                                       class="text-decoration-none text-success">
                                        @portfolio.CreatorName
                                    </a>

                                    <span class="mx-2">•</span>

                                    @(portfolio.CreatedAt.ToLocalTime().ToString("yyyy/MM/dd"))
                                </div>

                            </div>
                        </a>

                    </div>
                </div>
            }
        </div>

        <!-- 分頁 -->
        <nav class="mt-5">
            <ul class="pagination justify-content-center">

                @if (Model.HasPreviousPage)
                {
                    <li class="page-item">
                        <a class="page-link"
                           asp-action="Index"
                           asp-route-page="@(Model.Query.Page - 1)"
                           asp-route-keyword="@Model.Query.Keyword">
                            上一頁
                        </a>
                    </li>
                }

                @for (int i = 1; i <= Model.TotalPages; i++)
                {
                    <li class="page-item @(i == Model.Query.Page ? "active" : "")">
                        <a class="page-link"
                           asp-action="Index"
                           asp-route-page="@i"
                           asp-route-keyword="@Model.Query.Keyword">
                            @i
                        </a>
                    </li>
                }

                @if (Model.HasNextPage)
                {
                    <li class="page-item">
                        <a class="page-link"
                           asp-action="Index"
                           asp-route-page="@(Model.Query.Page + 1)"
                           asp-route-keyword="@Model.Query.Keyword">
                            下一頁
                        </a>
                    </li>
                }

            </ul>
        </nav>
    }

</div>

@model CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.CreatorPost.VMPostDetail
@using CraftDailyCorner.Extensions
@using CraftDailyCorner.Models.enums
@using CraftDailyCorner.ViewModels.Report

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = Model.Title;
}
@if (TempData["Success"] != null)
{
    <div class="alert alert-success">
        @TempData["Success"]
    </div>
}

@if (TempData["Warning"] != null)
{
    <div class="alert alert-warning">
        @TempData["Warning"]
    </div>
}

<div class="container py-5" style="max-width:900px;">

    <!-- 封面圖 -->
    <div class="mb-4">
        <img src="@Model.CoverImagePath"
             class="w-100 rounded shadow-sm"
             style="max-height:500px; object-fit:cover;" />
    </div>

    <!-- 標題 + 作者資訊 -->
    <div class="d-flex">
        <h2 class="fw-bold mb-0">
            <i class="bi bi-filter-right"></i>
            @Model.Title
            <i class="bi bi-filter-left"></i>
        </h2>
        <div class="h5 ms-4 mt-2">
            <!--心情反應與計數-->
            <partial name="_ReactionSummaryPartial"
                     model="Model.ReactionButton" />
        </div>
    </div>
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-end align-items-center text-muted small mt-2">
        <a asp-controller="Creator" asp-action="Profile" asp-route-id="@Model.CreatorID"
            class="text-decoration-none text-success">
            @Model.CreatorName
        </a>
        <i class="bi bi-caret-right-fill mx-2"></i>
        <span>@Model.CreatedAt.ToLocalTime().ToString("yyyy/MM/dd")</span>
    </div>

    <!-- 內文 -->
    <div class="mb-5 text-content" style="white-space:pre-line; line-height:1.9;">
        @Model.Content
    </div>

    

    <!--登入後才能使用-->
    <div class="mb-4">
        @if (User.Identity?.IsAuthenticated == true)
        {
            <div class="d-flex justify-content-end align-items-center flex-wrap gap-2">
                <!-- 留言按鈕 -->
                <button class="btn btn-dark btn-sm focus-none requires-auth"
                        data-bs-toggle="modal"
                        data-bs-target="#commentModal">
                    留言
                </button>

                <!-- 心情反應按鈕 -->
                <span class="big-effect requires-auth">
                    <partial name="_ReactionButtonPartial"
                             model="Model.ReactionButton" />
                </span>

                <!-- 檢舉 -->
                @if (!Model.IsOwner)
                {
                    <span class="requires-auth">
                        @if (Model.IsReportBanned)
                        {
                            <button type="button"
                                    class="btn btn-outline-secondary report-btn focus-none"
                                    disabled
                                    title="你目前已被暫停檢舉功能">
                                <i class="bi bi-flag report-icon-outline"></i>
                                <i class="bi bi-flag-fill report-icon-fill"></i>
                            </button>
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            <partial name="_ReportButtonPartial"
                                     model="new VMReport
                                     {
                                         ReportType = ReportTargetType.Post,
                                         TargetID = Model.PostID,
                                         IsReportBanned = Model.IsReportBanned,
                                         ReportBanUntil = Model.ReportBanUntil
                                     }" />
                                }
                    </span>
                }
            </div>

            @if (!Model.IsOwner && Model.IsReportBanned)
            {
                <div class="d-flex justify-content-end mt-1">
                    <div class="text-danger small">
                        <i class="bi bi-x-circle-fill me-1"></i>檢舉功能已停權至 @Model.ReportBanUntil?.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm")
                    </div>
                </div>
            }
        }
        else
        {
            <div class="d-flex justify-content-end align-items-center flex-wrap gap-2">
                <!-- 未登入：三個入口都存在，但都指向登入 Modal -->
                <button class="btn btn-dark btn-sm focus-none"
                        data-bs-toggle="modal"
                        data-bs-target="#loginModal">
                    留言
                </button>

                <span class="big-effect">
                    <button type="button"
                            class="btn btn-link text-secondary p-0 text-decoration-none focus-none"
                            data-bs-toggle="modal"
                            data-bs-target="#loginModal"
                            aria-label="登入後可使用心情反應">
                        <i class="bi bi-hand-thumbs-up-fill"></i>
                    </button>
                </span>

                @if (!Model.IsOwner)
                {
                    <button type="button"
                            class="btn btn-link text-secondary p-0 text-decoration-none focus-none"
                            data-bs-toggle="modal"
                            data-bs-target="#loginModal">
                        檢舉
                    </button>
                }
            </div>
        }
    </div>
    <hr style="border-top: 2px dashed #6c757d" />
    
    <!-- 留言列表 -->
    <div id="comment-container">
        @await Component.InvokeAsync("VCPostComment",
        new { postId = Model.PostID })
    </div>
    
</div>

<!-- 留言 Modal -->
<div class="modal fade" id="commentModal" tabindex="-1">
    <div class="modal-dialog">
        <div class="modal-content">

            <div class="modal-header">
                <h5 class="modal-title">新增留言</h5>
                <button type="button" class="btn-close"
                        data-bs-dismiss="modal"></button>
            </div>

                          class="form-control"
                          rows="4"
                          placeholder="請輸入留言內容"></textarea>
            </div>

            <div class="modal-footer">
                <button class="btn btn-dark"
                        id="btn-comment"
                        data-postid="@Model.PostID"
                        data-bs-dismiss="modal">
                    送出留言
                </button>
            </div>

    </div>

                   name="keyword"
                   value="@Model.Query.Keyword"
                   class="form-control"

                    class="btn btn-dark text-nowrap px-4">

            </button>

    {

        </div>

    {
        <div class="row g-4">

            @foreach (var item in Model.Posts)
            {
                <div class="col-md-6 col-lg-4">

                    <div class="card shadow-sm border-0 h-100 p-card">

                        <a asp-action="Detail"
                           asp-route-id="@item.PostID"
                           class="text-decoration-none text-dark">

                            <!-- 封面 -->

                                 class="card-img-top"
                                 style="height:240px; object-fit:cover;" />

                                <!-- 第1行：標題(左) + Reaction(右) -->
                                <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-2">

                                    <!-- 標題 -->
                                    <div class="fw-semibold h5 mb-0 text-truncate flex-grow-1 me-2">
                                        <i class="bi bi-filter-right"></i>
                                        @item.Title
                                        <i class="bi bi-filter-left"></i>
                                    </div>

                                    <!-- Reaction Summary -->
                                    <div class="ms-2 flex-shrink-0">
                                        <partial name="_ReactionSummaryPartial"
                                                 model="item.ReactionSummary" />
                                    </div>
                                </div>

                                <!-- 第2行：內文預覽(左) -->
                                <div class="text-muted small p-preview mb-2">
                                    @item.Preview
                                </div>

                                <!-- 第3行：留言數量 + 日期（靠右） -->
                                <div class="text-muted small text-end mt-auto">
                                    <a asp-controller="Creator" asp-action="Profile" asp-route-id="@item.CreatorID"
                                       class="text-decoration-none text-success">
                                        <i class="bi bi-person-fill"> </i>
                                        <span>
                                            @item.BrandName
                                        </span>
                                    </a>
                                    <span class="mx-2">•</span>
                                    <i class="bi bi-chat-left-dots"></i>
                                    @item.CommentCount 則留言

                                    <span class="mx-2">•</span>

                                    @(item.CreatedAt.ToLocalTime().ToString("yyyy/MM/dd"))
                                </div>

                            </div>
                        </a>

                    </div>

                </div>
            }

        </div>

        <!-- 分頁 -->
        <nav class="mt-5">
            <ul class="pagination justify-content-center">

                @if (Model.HasPreviousPage)
                {
                    <li class="page-item">
                        <a class="page-link"
                           asp-action="Index"
                           asp-route-page="@(Model.Query.Page - 1)"
                           asp-route-keyword="@Model.Query.Keyword">
                            上一頁
                        </a>
                    </li>
                }

                @for (int i = 1; i <= Model.TotalPages; i++)
                {
                    <li class="page-item @(i == Model.Query.Page ? "active" : "")">
                        <a class="page-link"
                           asp-action="Index"
                           asp-route-page="@i"
                           asp-route-keyword="@Model.Query.Keyword">
                            @i
                        </a>
                    </li>
                }

                @if (Model.HasNextPage)
                {
                    <li class="page-item">
                        <a class="page-link"
                           asp-action="Index"
                           asp-route-page="@(Model.Query.Page + 1)"
                           asp-route-keyword="@Model.Query.Keyword">
                            下一頁
                        </a>
                    </li>
                }

            </ul>
        </nav>
    }

</div>

FILE: CraftDailyCorner\Views\Products\Index.cshtml
